# 🕸️ 图神经网络与知识图谱推荐 (Graph & Knowledge-Aware Recommendation)

> **研究范畴**：LightGCN 谱图卷积改进、超图推荐 (Hypergraph)、异构图与知识图谱推理 (KGRec)

当前方向共收录 **475** 篇顶会/顶刊论文解读：

🔥 **高频交叉主题：** 多行为推荐 ×22 · 对比学习 ×22 · 多模态推荐 ×18 · 大语言模型推荐 ×17 · 去偏公平与可信推荐 ×16 · 生成式推荐 ×11 · 图神经网络推荐 ×9 · LightGCN ×8

<dl data-paper-list>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2021&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;特征交叉筛选&quot;,&quot;L0正则&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/detecting_beneficial_feature_interactions_for_recommender_systems/">Detecting Beneficial Feature Interactions for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>L0-SIGN用L0边预测筛选有益二阶交叉，再由SIGN聚合；Frappe ACC较最强基线提升2.50%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2021&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;会话推荐&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2021/sequential_rec/graph_enhanced_multi_task_learning_of_multi_level_transition_dynamics_for_s/">Graph-Enhanced Multi-Task Learning of Multi-Level Transition Dynamics for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MTD 用位置感知注意力建模会话内转移、用全局图编码器+互信息最大化建模跨会话转移，多任务联合学习，三数据集 Pre@10 最高提升 4.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2021&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多类型交互图&quot;,&quot;互信息图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2021/social_group_rec/knowledge_aware_coupled_graph_neural_network_for_social_recommendation/">Knowledge-aware Coupled Graph Neural Network for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KCGN，用多类型交互图 + 相对时间编码建模动态交互异质性，并以互信息估计对齐用户/物品孪生图的高阶结构；Leave-One-Out + 99 负采样协议下 Epinions HR@10 提升 4.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为建模&quot;,&quot;图Transformer&quot;,&quot;知识图谱&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2021/graph_rec/knowledge_enhanced_hierarchical_graph_transformer_network_for_multi_behavio/">Knowledge-Enhanced Hierarchical Graph Transformer Network for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>时间感知图Transformer融合多行为与知识图谱，Leave-One-Out+99负采样评测，Retail HR@10相对提升23.1%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何 Poincaré Ball&quot;,&quot;知识图谱注意力聚合&quot;,&quot;双层优化自适应正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2021/graph_rec/knowledge_enhanced_top_k_recommendation_in_poincar_ball/">Knowledge-Enhanced Top-K Recommendation in Poincaré Ball</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户/物品/KG 实体关系嵌入 Poincaré 球，用双曲注意力+Einstein 中点聚合邻居，并以双层优化自适应学习正则强度，NDCG@20 提升 3.46%–16.70%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;轨迹式元学习&quot;,&quot;时序动态建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2022/sequential_rec/leaping_through_time_with_gradient_based_adaptation_for_recommendation/">Leaping Through Time with Gradient-based Adaptation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用轨迹式元学习 Leap 替代 RNN 建模时序动态：GTL 抓跨时段共性、OTL 抓短期趋势，时间切分+1:99 采样排序下 HR@1 最高相对提升 18.59%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐&quot;,&quot;意图解耦&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2022/graph_rec/multi_view_intent_disentangle_graph_networks_for_bundle_recommendation/">Multi-view Intent Disentangle Graph Networks for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用邻域路由在全局/局部双视图上解耦用户 K 个意图，并以意图级 InfoNCE 对齐，NetEase 上 Recall@20 相对最强基线提升 38.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/排序&quot;,&quot;定向拓扑攻击&quot;,&quot;节点掩码&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_item_promotion_in_gnn_based_collaborative_filtering_a_masked_tar/">Revisiting Item Promotion in GNN-based Collaborative Filtering: A Masked Targeted Topological Attack Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将GNN协同过滤物品推广重述为定向拓扑攻击，以节点掩码降噪和目标列梯度实现小预算高效攻击；Gowalla/Yelp2018全库top-50命中显著超越启发式基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;傅里叶变换&quot;,&quot;过平滑缓解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/sequential_rec/an_attentive_inductive_bias_for_sequential_recommendation_beyond_the_self_a/">An Attentive Inductive Bias for Sequential Recommendation beyond the Self-Attention</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示自注意力低通滤波导致过平滑，用 DFT 注入注意力归纳偏置并重缩放频率，6 数据集全库排序平均提升约 6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Meta-path Prompt&quot;,&quot;路径去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/llm_rec/exploring_large_language_model_for_graph_data_understanding_in_online_job_r/">Exploring Large Language Model for Graph Data Understanding in Online Job Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 meta-path prompt 把异构行为图翻译成自然语言供 LLM 理解，并设计路径打乱/软选择器消除路径权重与位置偏置，AUC 最高提升 29.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/配料替代&quot;,&quot;FoodKG&quot;,&quot;水足迹&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/graph_rec/knowledge_powered_recommendation_for_an_improved_diet_water_footprint/">Knowledge-Powered Recommendation for an Improved Diet Water Footprint</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>AAAI 2024 知识图谱推荐系统演示：以 FoodKG 对齐营养与水足迹，按水足迹排序替代配料，案例水足迹降约74%，但无离线基准与消融。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图学习&quot;,&quot;解耦表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/multimodal_rec/lgmrec_local_and_global_graph_learning_for_multimodal_recommendation/">LGMRec: Local and Global Graph Learning for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>解耦协同/模态局部图表征并用模态超图捕获全局兴趣，Amazon 三数据集全库排序 R@20 最高提升 11.44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦图神经网络&quot;,&quot;群体公平&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/no_prejudice_fair_federated_graph_neural_networks_for_personalized_recommen/">No Prejudice! Fair Federated Graph Neural Networks for Personalized Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在联邦图推荐中以隐私保护的归纳式图扩展+LDP，把群体公平正则转化为梯度缩放因子，RMSE 差异降 47%–99%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱域图卷积&quot;,&quot;互补物品推荐&quot;,&quot;两阶段注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/graph_rec/spectral_based_graph_neural_networks_for_complementary_item_recommendation/">Spectral-Based Graph Neural Networks for Complementary Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>谱域解耦互补关系为低频相关性与中频差异性，用低通/中通 GCN 滤波器加两阶段注意力建模，四个 Amazon 数据集 HR@10 提升 4.4%–14.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;relevant-visit selection&quot;,&quot;target-aware attention&quot;,&quot;用药推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/sequential_rec/vita_carefully_chosen_and_weighted_less_is_better_in_medication_recommendat/">VITA: &#x27;Carefully Chosen and Weighted Less&#x27; Is Better in Medication Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可微 Gumbel 选访 + 目标感知注意力替代 RNN 顺序聚合，仅聚合与当前就诊相关的历史；MIMIC 上 Jaccard 相对最优基线提升 3.83%/5.56%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图神经架构搜索&quot;,&quot;行为重要性加权&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/behavior_importance_aware_graph_neural_architecture_search_for_cross_domain/">Behavior Importance-Aware Graph Neural Architecture Search for Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用一次性免重训超网为各域定制 GNN 架构，并以双层隐式梯度学习源域行为重要性权重，Amazon 与微信广告数据 AUC 最高提升 10.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;拓扑剪枝&quot;,&quot;邻域对齐损失&quot;,&quot;MLP替代GCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/multimodal_rec/beyond_graph_convolution_multimodal_recommendation_with_topology_aware_mlps/">Beyond Graph Convolution: Multimodal Recommendation with Topology-aware MLPs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用拓扑剪枝+邻域对齐损失替代GCN消息传递，以MLP建模多模态物品关系，全库排序下Recall@10最高提升7.53%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户侧知识图谱&quot;,&quot;图掩码自编码器&quot;,&quot;跨域对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/bridging_the_user_side_knowledge_gap_in_knowledge_aware_recommendations_wit/">Bridging the User-side Knowledge Gap in Knowledge-aware Recommendations with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 推断用户兴趣并结构化为 CIKG 异构图，配合兴趣重建与跨域对比学习去噪，在三个数据集全库排序下 R@100 最高提升 9.31%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跳过行为建模&quot;,&quot;双层图卷积&quot;,&quot;分层排序损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/multimodal_rec/exploiting_fine_grained_skip_behaviors_for_micro_video_recommendation/">Exploiting Fine-Grained Skip Behaviors for Micro-Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将短视频跳过行为细分为高正/低正/负三级，构建双层正图与分层 BPR 损失，两数据集八项指标全面超越 FRAME 等基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果去混杂&quot;,&quot;反事实曝光干预&quot;,&quot;多样性推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/diversity_recommendation_via_causal_deconfounding_of_co_purchase_relations_/">Diversity Recommendation via Causal Deconfounding of Co-purchase Relations and Counterfactual Exposure</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用去混杂非对称共购图 UACR 与反事实曝光提权插件，在 LightGCN 上同时提升多样性与准确率，F_β 最优且训练开销仅高 12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;检索增强生成&quot;,&quot;图注意力网络&quot;,&quot;空间相关性回灌&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/graph_rec/graph_neural_field_with_spatial_correlation_augmentation_for_hrtf_personali/">Graph Neural Field with Spatial-Correlation Augmentation for HRTF Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以受试者检索图 + GAT 编解码生成个性化 HRTF，再用方向图 GNN 做空间一致性回灌，3 方向测量下 LSD 降至 3.60 dB。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端序列决策&quot;,&quot;学习路径推荐&quot;,&quot;GraphRAG&quot;,&quot;判别学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/graph_rec/graphrag_induced_dual_knowledge_structure_graphs_for_personalized_learning_/">GraphRAG-Induced Dual Knowledge Structure Graphs for Personalized Learning Path Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 TextGrad+EDU-GraphRAG 自动合成先决/相似双知识结构图，再用 PPO 三智能体生成学习路径，仿真协议下最长路径提升约 29%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;排序&quot;,&quot;双曲图卷积&quot;,&quot;Mamba-MoE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/sequential_rec/hyperbolic_enhanced_mixture_of_experts_mamba_for_sequential_recommendation/">Hyperbolic-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts Mamba for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双曲GCN+VGAE重构物品图，RoPE-MoE Mamba动态建模；四数据集HR/NDCG最高提升6.12%，但划分/排序协议未明。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;残差扩散&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/generative_rec/modeling_item_level_dynamic_variability_with_residual_diffusion_for_bundle_/">Modeling Item-Level Dynamic Variability with Residual Diffusion for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用残差扩散在潜空间重建捆绑包item级表征，模型无关地适配B-I归属动态变化，Recall/NDCG最高提升约23%，训练开销仅增4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;超图学习&quot;,&quot;参数分裂&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/multifaceted_scenario_aware_hypergraph_learning_for_next_poi_recommendation/">Multifaceted Scenario-Aware Hypergraph Learning for Next POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出场景拆分式多视图超图与自适应参数分裂，在 NYC/TKY/Gowalla 上按场景提升 Next-POI 排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;动态图推荐&quot;,&quot;任务感知子图检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/graph_rec/task_aware_retrieval_augmentation_for_dynamic_recommendation/">Task-Aware Retrieval Augmentation for Dynamic Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用任务感知评估机制自动标注历史子图收益，并以图 Transformer 打分检索融合，缓解动态推荐预训练-微调时序泛化鸿沟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2021&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;多轮对话式推荐（端到端生成）&quot;,&quot;树状知识图谱推理&quot;,&quot;对话动作（Dialog Act）序列化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/agent_conversational_rec/cr_walker_tree_structured_graph_reasoning_and_dialog_acts_for_conversationa/">CR-Walker: Tree-Structured Graph Reasoning and Dialog Acts for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 walker cell 在 KG 上做两跳树状推理，并把推理树前序线性化为对话动作来约束 GPT-2 生成；ReDial 覆盖率 14.7、知识 F1 47.7，人工评测四项全面超越人类参考答案。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2021&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;知识图谱多跳推理&quot;,&quot;强化学习策略搜索&quot;,&quot;路径片段融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/agent_conversational_rec/crfr_improving_conversational_recommender_systems_via_flexible_fragments_re/">CRFR: Improving Conversational Recommender Systems via Flexible Fragments Reasoning on Knowledge Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用强化学习在物品/概念双知识图谱上做显式多跳推理，以柔性路径片段而非完整路径增强推荐与回复生成，ReDial R@10 相对提升 10.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱路径推理&quot;,&quot;神经逻辑规则&quot;,&quot;解释忠实性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/graph_rec/faithfully_explainable_recommendation_via_neural_logic_reasoning/">Faithfully Explainable Recommendation via Neural Logic Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用马尔可夫逻辑规则+EM 迭代联合训练 KG 编码器与规则权重，并以个性化规则分引导路径推理，兼顾推荐精度与解释忠实度（JS 散度、用户调研双验证）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;Findings of EMNLP&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;多子目标规划&quot;,&quot;知识图谱&quot;,&quot;知识增强生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/agent_conversational_rec/kers_a_knowledge_enhanced_framework_for_recommendation_dialog_systems_with_/">KERS: A Knowledge-Enhanced Framework for Recommendation Dialog Systems with Multiple Subgoals</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以子目标规划筛选知识，并用顺序注意力、知识门控与词典增强抑制噪声，DuRecDial 上 F1 与知识 F1 显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;Findings of EMNLP 2021&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回/粗排&quot;,&quot;三塔相关性建模&quot;,&quot;竞价图&quot;,&quot;解耦图编码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/leveraging_bidding_graphs_for_advertiser_aware_relevance_modeling_in_sponso/">Leveraging Bidding Graphs for Advertiser-Aware Relevance Modeling in Sponsored Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将竞价图引入 query-keyword-advertiser 三塔相关性建模，ROC-AUC 0.801 较 TextGNN 提升 0.4 个点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2021 Findings&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/粗排&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;,&quot;跨选择门控&quot;,&quot;层次聚类图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2021/graph_rec/neural_news_recommendation_with_collaborative_news_encoding_and_structural_/">Neural News Recommendation with Collaborative News Encoding and Structural User Encoding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CNE+SUE，用标题-内容跨选择/交叉注意力和层次聚类图编码用户历史；在 200K-MIND 的 AUC 69.55，较最佳基线 +0.92。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;知识图谱路径推理&quot;,&quot;双向模仿学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/agent_conversational_rec/aligning_recommendation_and_conversation_via_dual_imitation/">Aligning Recommendation and Conversation via Dual Imitation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 KG 推荐路径与对话兴趣迁移路径的双向模仿学习紧耦合推荐与对话，OpenDialKG 上 Recall@1 相对最强基线提升 14.1%–62.3%，并显著提升解释连贯性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2022 Findings&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排（印象内新闻排序）&quot;,&quot;语义增强图 SAG&quot;,&quot;双图交互注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/graph_rec/digat_modeling_news_recommendation_with_dual_graph_interaction/">DIGAT: Modeling News Recommendation with Dual-Graph Interaction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用语义增强图 SAG 补足单条候选新闻语义，并以双图交互注意力迭代融合新闻图与用户图，MIND-large AUC 70.08、nDCG@5 提升 3.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2023&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;召回/生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;树结构推理&quot;,&quot;知识图谱&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2023/agent_conversational_rec/trea_tree_structure_reasoning_schema_for_conversational_recommendation/">TREA: Tree-Structure Reasoning Schema for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多层级推理树显式建模实体因果链，配合分支隔离/语义对齐损失，ReDial/TG-ReDial 全实体 R@50 提升 1.7%/41.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;高阶交互&quot;,&quot;全词嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/enhancing_high_order_interaction_awareness_in_llm_based_recommender_model/">Enhancing High-order Interaction Awareness in LLM-based Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用LightGCN随机特征传播生成图感知全词嵌入并注入T5，配合免训练重排；Leave-One-Out下直接与序列推荐均显著领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端（对话-推荐联合建模）&quot;,&quot;多通道超图&quot;,&quot;多兴趣自监督学习&quot;,&quot;马太效应缓解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/agent_conversational_rec/mitigating_matthew_effect_multi_hypergraph_boosted_multi_interest_self_supe/">Mitigating Matthew Effect: Multi-Hypergraph Boosted Multi-Interest Self-Supervised Learning for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以「物品/实体/词 × group/joint/purchase」九路 motif 超图学多层级多兴趣并经 InfoNCE 对齐，REDIAL 上 R@10 提升 11.5% 且覆盖率最高、推荐流行度最低。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)&quot;,&quot;解释生成/推荐后处理&quot;,&quot;协同指令微调&quot;,&quot;Mixture-of-Experts Adapter&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/xrec_large_language_models_for_explainable_recommendation/">XRec: Large Language Models for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出模型无关 XRec，用 LightGCN 协同分词器与 MoE 适配器把协同信号注入 LLM 各层，在三数据集语义解释指标上显著优于 ID-based 基线且 USR≈1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Graph RAG&quot;,&quot;Multi-LoRA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/kerl_knowledge_enhanced_personalized_recipe_recommendation_using_large_lang/">KERL: Knowledge-Enhanced Personalized Recipe Recommendation using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 FoodKG 子图作上下文 + 多 LoRA 适配器，统一完成约束食谱推荐、烹饪步骤生成与微量营养素估计，KGQA 基准 F1 达 0.973。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;KG-RAG&quot;,&quot;图软提示&quot;,&quot;选择性检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/knowledge_graph_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用跳场知识子图索引+流行度选择性检索+GNN软提示，将KG结构知识注入冻结LLM，MovieLens-1M ACC 相对提升58.6%且推理仅慢0.14s</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;持续图记忆&quot;,&quot;测试时自适应&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/graph_rec/continual_graph_memory_for_adaptive_recommendation_under_intent_drift/">Continual Graph Memory for Adaptive Recommendation under Intent Drift</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将知识图谱视为可写双记忆（语义图记忆+情景教训记忆），冻结参数下仅靠质量门控图编辑实现意图漂移自适应重排。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LoRA 参数高效微调&quot;,&quot;图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/graphlora_structure_aware_low_rank_adaptation_for_large_language_model_reco/">GraphLoRA: Structure-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Model Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将可训练 GNN 消息传递嵌入 LoRA 瓶颈（A 与 B 之间），让协同拓扑在参数空间传播；在 ML-1M/Amazon-Book 上以 r=8 的极小参数预算超越 CoRA 等 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;协同记忆图&quot;,&quot;架构解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/memrec_collaborative_memory_augmented_agentic_recommender_system/">MemRec: Collaborative Memory-Augmented Agentic Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将记忆管理从推理中架构解耦，用轻量 LM_Mem 在协同记忆图上做零样本规则剪枝与异步传播，仅向重型 LLM_Rec 注入蒸馏后的高信号协同上下文，四基准全库排序 H@1 最高 +28.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM 2021&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图结构学习&quot;,&quot;模态自适应图融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2021/multimodal_rec/mining_latent_structures_for_multimedia_recommendation/">Mining Latent Structures for Multimedia Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模态感知图结构学习从多模态特征中挖掘物品-物品潜在语义图，经GCN注入ID嵌入，Clothing Recall@20 相对 SOTA 提升 12.5%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图结构冻结&quot;,&quot;度数敏感边剪枝&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/multimodal_rec/a_tale_of_two_graphs_freezing_and_denoising_graph_structures_for_multimodal/">A Tale of Two Graphs: Freezing and Denoising Graph Structures for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结多模态物品-物品图并对用户-物品图做度数敏感剪枝去噪，全排序协议下相对 LATTICE 平均提升 19.07%，内存最高降 6×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;游玩时长建模&quot;,&quot;随机游走多样性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2025/multimodal_rec/dual_phase_playtime_guided_recommendation_interest_intensity_exploration_an/">Dual-Phase Playtime-guided Recommendation: Interest Intensity Exploration and Multimodal Random Walks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双 Beta 分布解耦游玩时长强弱兴趣，并以多模态随机游走做跨类目探索，在 Steam 数据上同时提升准确率与覆盖度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;物品-物品图&quot;,&quot;三元闭包重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2026/multimodal_rec/one_graph_multiple_gains_single_high_quality_item_item_graph_for_multimodal/">One Graph, Multiple Gains: Single High-Quality Item-Item Graph for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建一次高质量物品-物品图，经三元闭包重加权后复用于表征、交互图与优化三阶段，四数据集全指标SOTA且训练更快更省显存。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质三部图&quot;,&quot;图去噪子图采样&quot;,&quot;Gumbel-Softmax 可微离散采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/concept_aware_denoising_graph_neural_network_for_micro_video_recommendation/">Concept-Aware Denoising Graph Neural Network for Micro-Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用标题/评论抽取概念构建用户-视频-概念三部图，GRU+Gumbel-Softmax 个性化去噪子图，UAUC 相对 GAT 提升 8.3%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱蒸馏&quot;,&quot;条件注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/conditional_attention_networks_for_distilling_knowledge_graphs_in_recommend/">Conditional Graph Attention Networks for Distilling and Refining Knowledge Graphs in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>知识感知注意力蒸馏目标特定子图 + 局部条件注意力精炼，Leave-One-Out 采样排序 Hit@10 相对提升约 10%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;连续时间编码&quot;,&quot;时序图协同注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/sequential_rec/continuous_time_sequential_recommendation_with_temporal_graph_collaborative/">Continuous-Time Sequential Recommendation with Temporal Graph Collaborative Transformer</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构造连续时间二分图，用时间核编码+协同注意力统一序列模式与跨用户时序协同信号，Recall@10 平均绝对提升 22.5%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;可微采样 Gumbel-Softmax&quot;,&quot;关系感知注意力聚合&quot;,&quot;知识图谱去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/dskreg_differentiable_sampling_on_knowledge_graph_for_recommendation_with_r/">DSKReG: Differentiable Sampling on Knowledge Graph for Recommendation with Relational GNN</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 co-interact 关系图并用 Gumbel-Softmax 可微采样 top-K 相关邻居，端到端去噪 KG 噪声；全库排序下 NDCG@20 最高提升 37.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间&quot;,&quot;图卷积网络&quot;,&quot;Lorentz 模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/fully_hyperbolic_graph_convolution_network_for_recommendation/">Fully Hyperbolic Graph Convolution Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将嵌入、邻域聚合、激活校准与测地距离打分全部置于 Lorentz 双曲空间，避免切空间近似误差，全库排序 R@20 相对 LightGCN 提升 7%–9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图信号处理与低通滤波&quot;,&quot;闭式解免训练协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/how_powerful_is_graph_convolution_for_recommendation/">How Powerful is Graph Convolution for Recommendation?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用图信号处理证明 LightGCN 本质是低通滤波，导出无限维嵌入闭式解，并提出免训练 GF-CF 基线，Amazon-book 上 Recall@20 较 LightGCN 提升 72.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2021&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间超图卷积&quot;,&quot;自监督预训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/sequential_rec/hyperbolic_hypergraphs_for_sequential_recommendation/">Hyperbolic Hypergraphs for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用月度/季度/年度分层双曲超图卷积 + 三项自监督预训练（含超边链接预测）建模序列推荐，在 AMT/Goodreads 采样排序下 NDCG@1 最高提 31.61%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2021&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/端到端&quot;,&quot;时间切片图&quot;,&quot;时序点过程&quot;,&quot;双重动态表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/sequential_rec/learning_dual_dynamic_representations_on_time_sliced_user_item_interaction_/">Learning Dual Dynamic Representations on Time-Sliced User-Item Interaction Graphs for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>按等长时间切片构建全局用户-物品二部图，用切片 GNN + 双侧 GRU 建模用户/物品双重动态，再以时序点过程辅助任务补偿离散化时间损失；1:100 采样排序下 NDCG@10 相对 SCoRe 提升 3.4%–10.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;神经微分方程 NODE&quot;,&quot;可学习层组合时间&quot;,&quot;高阶 ODE 求解器与稠密连接&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/lt_ocf_learnable_time_ode_based_collaborative_filtering/">LT-OCF: Learnable-Time ODE-based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LightGCN 的离散层传播改写为用户/商品双共演化神经常微分方程，并学习层组合时间点，在三个基准的全库排序评测上稳定超越 LightGCN。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;元路径异质图神经网络&quot;,&quot;图互信息自监督学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/social_group_rec/social_recommendation_with_self_supervised_metagraph_informax_network/">Social Recommendation with Self-Supervised Metagraph Informax Network</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出元路径异质图编码 + 跨元路径注意力 + 子结构感知互信息自监督的 SMIN，在 Ciao/Epinions/Yelp 的 1:99 采样排序上较 NGCF+ 提升约 2.4% HR@10。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;无穷层消息传递极限&quot;,&quot;约束损失&quot;,&quot;物品共现图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2021/graph_rec/ultragcn_ultra_simplification_of_graph_convolutional_networks_for_recommend/">UltraGCN: Ultra Simplification of Graph Convolutional Networks for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用约束损失直接逼近无穷层消息传递的收敛态，无显式传播、仅 75 轮收敛，Recall@20 较 DGCF 提升 61.4% 且训练加速 14 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;免数据增强图对比学习&quot;,&quot;自适应层融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/self_supervised_denoising/adaptive_fusion_self_supervised_learning_for_recommendation/">Adaptive Fusion Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>AFGCL以免数据增强的传播层自适应融合与交互锚点对比目标，提升稀疏推荐精度并降低训练成本。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异构图神经网络&quot;,&quot;双查询注意力融合&quot;,&quot;距离协方差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/aminergnn_heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_paper_click_through_rate_p/">AMinerGNN: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Paper Click-through Rate Prediction with Fusion Query</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 user-paper-keyword 异构图统一表征，用距离协方差注意力动态融合 userID 与 keyword 双查询，AMiner_KRS 上 AUC 0.899（较 MGDSPR +3.2%），线上 UV-CTR 提升 9.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表征学习&quot;,&quot;强化学习元路径选择&quot;,&quot;异质信息网络&quot;,&quot;元路径推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/graph_rec/automatic_meta_path_discovery_for_effective_graph_based_recommendation/">Automatic Meta-Path Discovery for Effective Graph-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出RMS强化学习元路径自动选择与HRec注意力聚合推荐器；在Yelp/Douban/TCL的Leave-One-Out、1正+499负采样排序下，HR@3较最佳基线最高提升12.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权 IPS&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;邻居聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_neighbor_aggregation_for_graph_neural_network_in_recommender_syst/">Debiasing Neighbor Aggregation for Graph Neural Network in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把逆倾向权重下推进 GNN 邻居聚合，用加权随机游走拉普拉斯归一化，推理期免训练重聚合即可在 Alexa 上提升 NDCG@5 14.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Neural ODE&quot;,&quot;连续时间动态图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/sequential_rec/evolutionary_preference_learning_via_graph_nested_gru_ode_for_session_based/">Evolutionary Preference Learning via Graph Nested GRU ODE for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将会话建模为连续时间动态图，以GNG-ODE与t-Alignment学习偏好连续演化，全库排序下MRR@20最高提升6.05%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图特征迁移&quot;,&quot;多模态语义链接&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/cold_start_rec/gift_graph_guided_feature_transfer_for_cold_start_video_click_through_rate_/">GIFT: Graph-guIded Feature Transfer for Cold-Start Video Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建物理+多模态语义异构链接，用 metapath 引导的 target attention 把热视频的 ID 表征与统计特征迁移给新视频，淘宝线上 CTR 提升 6.82%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;树形元结构 TMS&quot;,&quot;Tree2vec&quot;,&quot;异构图嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/cross_domain_multi_scenario/gres_graphical_cross_domain_recommendation_for_supply_chain_platform/">GReS: Graphical Cross-domain Recommendation for Supply Chain Platform</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>为餐饮供应链平台设计「菜品-类目-食材」三层树形元结构与 Tree2vec(GCN+BERT) 编码，配合异构图 Node2vec 与元素级注意力融合，HR@10 相对最强基线提升 7.35%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;上下文漂移解耦&quot;,&quot;随机游走图嵌入&quot;,&quot;多头交互注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/graph_rec/hysage_a_hybrid_static_and_adaptive_graph_embedding_network_for_context_dri/">HySAGE: A Hybrid Static and Adaptive Graph Embedding Network for Context-Drifting Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>解耦静态交互图嵌入与漂移上下文自适应嵌入，注意力交互融合，全库未交互排序下 HR@10 最高提升 28.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回（端到端 Next-Item 预测）&quot;,&quot;隐式会话上下文&quot;,&quot;图聚类与上下文预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/sequential_rec/implicit_session_contexts_for_next_item_recommendations/">Implicit Session Contexts for Next-Item Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>会话-物品图 GraphSAGE 嵌入 + K-means 聚类生成隐式会话上下文，用双 Bi-LSTM 上下文预测器作先验特征，Foursquare MRR 相对最优基线 +36.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多层交互对比学习&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;非局部图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/graph_rec/improving_knowledge_aware_recommendation_with_multi_level_interactive_contr/">Improving Knowledge-aware Recommendation with Multi-level Interactive Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KGIC：以 GNN 层为视图做图内 CF-KG 与图间局部-非局部两层交互对比，三数据集 AUC 相对提升 0.67%–3.35%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回(端到端联邦训练)&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;用户聚类&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/personalized_federated_recommendation_via_joint_representation_learning_use/">Personalized Federated Recommendation via Joint Representation Learning, User Clustering, and Model Adaptation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 PerFedRec，用联邦 GNN 联合学习表征、用户聚类与三级模型自适应，实现个性化联邦推荐并降低通信开销，HR@10 较 FedAvg 平均提升 29.47%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;子图级图神经网络&quot;,&quot;捆绑包推荐&quot;,&quot;迁移推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/graph_rec/suger_a_subgraph_based_graph_convolutional_network_method_for_bundle_recomm/">SUGER: A Subgraph-based Graph Convolutional Network Method for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以用户-捆绑包为中心抽取异质 k 跳子图，用两级 RGCN 图级编码器打分，并删除中心边消除标签泄漏；Recall@20 在 Youshu/Netease 上相对 BGCN 提升 39%/90%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;低秩分解&quot;,&quot;图卷积简化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/graph_rec/svd_gcn_a_simplified_graph_convolution_paradigm_for_recommendation/">SVD-GCN: A Simplified Graph Convolution Paradigm for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用截断 SVD 替代 GCN 邻域聚合，仅取 K 个奇异向量并重归一化，全库排序精度提升且百倍加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;用户侧推荐 (User-side RecSys)&quot;,&quot;度量恢复 (Metric Recovery)&quot;,&quot;公平性公理化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_principled_user_side_recommender_systems/">Towards Principled User-side Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 k-NN 度量恢复理论证明用户侧推荐可行，并提出满足一致性/可靠性/局部性三公理的 Consul 算法，通信开销降低约 8–19 倍且性能近 Oracle。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排（推理期图增广）&quot;,&quot;反事实图增广&quot;,&quot;人口统计学均等&quot;,&quot;二部图扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/counterfactual_graph_augmentation_for_consumer_unfairness_mitigation_in_rec/">Counterfactual Graph Augmentation for Consumer Unfairness Mitigation in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 B 维可微二值边选择向量对二部图做反事实增广，以 ApproxNDCG 组间差损失只抬升劣势群体效用，让“解释不公平”直接变成“缓解不公平”。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（用户表征学习）&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私 LDP&quot;,&quot;去中心化图神经网络&quot;,&quot;超图兴趣凝聚&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/decentralized_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recommendation/">Decentralized Graph Neural Network for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>DGREC 用「用户内物品超图兴趣凝聚 + 邻域 1-bit 梯度编码共享」，构建首个去中心化 GNN 推荐框架，满足 (1.5,ε)-RDP 且解码无偏，全库排序 recall@20 较最佳隐私基线提升 7.6%–25.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对齐与一致性&quot;,&quot;高阶邻域聚合&quot;,&quot;轻量图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/graph_rec/graph_based_alignment_and_uniformity_for_recommendation/">Graph-based Alignment and Uniformity for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以高阶邻域聚合压缩稠密表示并对齐用户/物品，层间池化缓解稀疏，Office R@20 较 DirectAU 提升 16.64%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;拓扑接近度 Topological Closeness&quot;,&quot;GNN 表达性理论&quot;,&quot;免学习推荐算法&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/graph_rec/how_expressive_are_graph_neural_networks_in_recommendation/">How Expressive are Graph Neural Networks in Recommendation?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出三层 GNN 表达性度量框架，用链路级「拓扑接近度」替代图同构度量，并给出免学习 GTE 算法与六数据集全库排序验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;嵌入解耦&quot;,&quot;随机游走域对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/multi_domain_recommendation_with_embedding_disentangling_and_domain_alignme/">Multi-domain Recommendation with Embedding Disentangling and Domain Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>EDDA 在模型与嵌入双层解耦域间/域内知识，并用随机游走停靠向量对齐跨域相似节点，采样排序下 AUC 最高 +7.6%、Recall +41.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多粒度注意力&quot;,&quot;群组偏好聚合&quot;,&quot;组图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/social_group_rec/multi_granularity_attention_model_for_group_recommendation/">Multi-Granularity Attention Model for Group Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用子集/群组/超集三级粒度注意力（K-Means 子集聚类 + 组图 GCN + 自注意力融合）挖掘稀疏用户隐偏好，离线 HR/NDCG 全面领先，线上 CTR +1.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;投影解耦&quot;,&quot;多专家网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/multi_task_reranking/parallel_knowledge_enhancement_based_framework_for_multi_behavior_recommend/">Parallel Knowledge Enhancement based Framework for Multi-behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以并行流知识投影增强级联多行为 GNN，并用投影解耦多专家消除 MTL 梯度冲突，三数据集 HR@10 最高提升 95.16%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;超图推荐&quot;,&quot;一致性感知增强&quot;,&quot;购物篮推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/self_supervised_denoising/robust_basket_recommendation_via_noise_tolerated_graph_contrastive_learning/">Robust Basket Recommendation via Noise-tolerated Graph Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>BNCL 以双行为图/超图与一致性感知对比学习抑制购物篮噪声，全库排序下 R@60 最高 21.10。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据注入(训练期投毒，影响召回与排序)&quot;,&quot;Shilling Attack&quot;,&quot;图节点生成&quot;,&quot;影响函数&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/single_user_injection_for_invisible_shilling_attack_against_recommender_sys/">Single-User Injection for Invisible Shilling Attack against Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次研究仅注入 1 个虚假用户画像的 shilling 攻击，将投毒建模为二部图节点生成（毒性特征生成 + Gumbel-Top-K 连边），单用户 HR@50 最高超最好基线 9.75 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多视图时空子图&quot;,&quot;实时兴趣门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/stgin_spatial_temporal_graph_interaction_network_for_large_scale_poi_recomm/">STGIN: Spatial-Temporal Graph Interaction Network for Large-scale POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>四视图时空子图+GAT聚合、实时序列门控融合，工业数据 HitRate@200 达 25.87%，线上 CTR +1.1%/RPM +6.3%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;商品知识图谱预训练&quot;,&quot;零样本物品表征&quot;,&quot;任务导向适配层&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/cold_start_rec/zero_shot_item_based_recommendation_via_multi_task_product_knowledge_graph_/">Zero-shot Item-based Recommendation via Multi-task Product Knowledge Graph Pre-Training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在商品知识图谱上多任务预训练 SGC 编码器并用 ToA 适配层微调，为无交互物品推断归纳式表征；Xmarket 18 国全库排序 NDCG@20 相对提升 18%–83%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排&quot;,&quot;互补知识图&quot;,&quot;E-E-I权重决策&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/llm_rec/breaking_the_barrier_utilizing_large_language_models_for_industrial_recomme/">Breaking the Barrier: Utilizing Large Language Models for Industrial Recommendation Systems through an Inferential Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CIKM 2024[LLM推荐] 用 LLM 离线构建互补知识图，结合 U2E2I 召回与 E-E-I 精排校准；在支付宝三场景 AUC 与线上转化显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;脑电信号&quot;,&quot;量子认知&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/graph_rec/quantum_cognition_inspired_eeg_based_recommendation_via_graph_neural_networ/">Quantum Cognition-Inspired EEG-based Recommendation via Graph Neural Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以滑窗、量子认知塌缩和连续/干扰双图 GCN 学习 EEG 实时思维，MBD 采样 P@10 较 BPR 最高提升 95.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤量化&quot;,&quot;量化感知训练&quot;,&quot;动态步长&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/collaborative_filtering/a_node_aware_dynamic_quantization_approach_for_graph_collaborative_filterin/">A Node-Aware Dynamic Quantization Approach for Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出节点感知动态量化 GNAQ，用参数共享学习每节点量化步长并以图消息传递更新，2-bit 下 Recall@10 平均提升 27.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;搜索增强推荐&quot;,&quot;向量量化码本&quot;,&quot;用户-码本图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/benefit_from_rich_tackling_search_interaction_sparsity_in_search_enhanced_r/">Benefit from Rich: Tackling Search Interaction Sparsity in Search Enhanced Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM+RQ-VAE 将用户搜索/推荐偏好离散为码本，构建 User-Code 图做消息传递，把搜索富用户信号传给稀疏用户，Qilin 上 HR@1 相对 UniSAR 提升 12.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;图聚合权重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/causality_aware_graph_aggregation_weight_estimator_for_popularity_debiasing/">Causality-aware Graph Aggregation Weight Estimator for Popularity Debiasing in Top-K Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将图聚合证明为后门调整，用 VAE 式编码器-解码器估计无偏聚合权重，配合动量更新，小众物品 Recall@20 提升约 20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;社交模块度最大化&quot;,&quot;特征互斥 HSIC&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/social_group_rec/community_aware_social_community_recommendation/">Community-Aware Social Community Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CASO，用模块度最大化、社交紧密度聚合与协同编码三路解耦用户表征，并以 HSIC 互斥与 KL 社区检测损失优化，在 6 个真实社交网络上 Full-Ranking 全面超越 9 个基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;增量推荐&quot;,&quot;时序状态演化&quot;,&quot;伪多任务学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/sequential_rec/cpmr_context_aware_incremental_sequential_recommendation_with_pseudo_multi_/">CPMR: Context-Aware Incremental Sequential Recommendation with Pseudo-Multi-Task Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出伪多任务学习范式，用历史图与上下文图双通道演化用户/物品时序状态，在四个数据集上 MRR 平均提升 30.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Fourier KAN&quot;,&quot;特征变换&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/graph_rec/enhancing_graph_collaborative_filtering_with_fourierkan_feature_transformat/">Enhancing Graph Collaborative Filtering with FourierKAN Feature Transformation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Fourier KAN 替代 NGCF 中被误删的交互特征变换 W2，在 7:1:2 时间切分全库排序下 R@20 最高提升 7.8%，且可作即插即用骨干。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;生成式数据增强&quot;,&quot;物品互补矩阵&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/self_supervised_denoising/generative_data_augmentation_in_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendati/">Generative Data Augmentation in Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 VAE 式生成器自适应生成噪声替代固定随机扰动构造对比视图，并引入物品互补矩阵，在三个稀疏数据集上 Full-Ranking 提升约 3–6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超复数代数&quot;,&quot;Prompt 补偿&quot;,&quot;自监督对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/multimodal_rec/hypercomplex_prompt_aware_multimodal_recommendation/">Hypercomplex Prompt-aware Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Cayley-Dickson 超复数多分量嵌入 + 超复数乘法非线性融合 + Prompt 动态补偿，在四个 Amazon 数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升约 13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图数据增广&quot;,&quot;HSIC 去偏&quot;,&quot;群体公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/improving_recommendation_fairness_via_graph_structure_and_representation_au/">Improving Recommendation Fairness via Graph Structure and Representation Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双先验假设定位敏感边与敏感特征，做图剪枝+特征掩码双增广，HSIC去偏+对比学习保效用，ML-1M 上 DP@10 降 3.4%、EO@10 降 7.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何&quot;,&quot;知识图谱去噪&quot;,&quot;流行度自适应融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/graph_rec/spark_adaptive_low_rank_knowledge_graph_modeling_in_hybrid_geometric_spaces/">SPARK: Adaptive Low-Rank Knowledge Graph Modeling in Hybrid Geometric Spaces for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Tucker 低秩去噪 KG、SVD 初始化双曲/欧氏并行 GNN，并按物品流行度自适应融合多源信号，长尾推荐 R@10 最高提升 10.8%。</p>
</dd>
