<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Folding-In&quot;,&quot;热启动&quot;,&quot;UltraGCN&quot;,&quot;低秩近似&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/graph_rec/ultra_fast_warm_start_solution_for_graph_recommendations/">Ultra Fast Warm Start Solution for Graph Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 SVD Folding-In 的闭式解迁移到 UltraGCN，用一次矩阵-向量乘精确更新热用户嵌入，比 SGD 微调快 30 倍且质量更优、覆盖率更高。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;图重连&quot;,&quot;环境条件建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/graph_rec/beyond_observed_auxiliary_relations_environment_conditioned_modeling_for_mu/">Beyond Observed Auxiliary Relations: Environment-Conditioned Modeling for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以辅助行为可观测性为环境条件，用去偏稠密化与目标引导剪枝双模块重连多行为图，隐藏目标物品 HR@10 最高提升 44.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱融合&quot;,&quot;对抗扰动不稳定性估计&quot;,&quot;自适应对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/graph_rec/do_all_nodes_benefit_equally_from_knowledge_graphs_adaptive_node_aware_kg_f/">Do All Nodes Benefit Equally from Knowledge Graphs? Adaptive Node-Aware KG Fusion for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以对抗扰动估计节点级 CF 不稳定性，自适应门控 KG 注入强度并配合自适应对比对齐，四数据集 Full-Ranking 平均排名第一。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;拉普拉斯位置编码&quot;,&quot;图谱结构信号&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/sequential_rec/enriching_sequential_recommendation_with_graph_laplacian_positional_embeddi/">Enriching Sequential Recommendation with Graph Laplacian Positional Embeddings</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用物品共现图归一化拉普拉斯特征向量冻结替换 SASRec 可学习位置嵌入，四基准多数指标提升且在线开销近零。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;曲率感知传播深度&quot;,&quot;过平滑&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/graph_rec/neural_tree_collaborative_filtering_rethinking_graph_collaborative_filterin/">Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering: Rethinking Graph Collaborative Filtering as Tree Collaborative Filtering with Curvature-Aware Propagation Depth</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以局部度不平衡曲率代理为每节点分配自适应传播深度，将 GCF 重释为节点根树集成，Full-Ranking 下 R@20 稳定超越 LightGCN 与 SSL 骨干。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多哈希嵌入&quot;,&quot;时序邻居采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/social_group_rec/scaling_graph_neural_networks_for_friend_recommendation_multi_hash_user_emb/">Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多哈希ID嵌入(表压缩&gt;98%)与时间戳排序CSR二分采样(O(log d+K))支撑194M用户/28B边GNN好友排序，线上好友添加+16%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;知识图谱路径推理&quot;,&quot;残差量化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/generative_rec/sprig_semantic_id_enhanced_paths_for_knowledge_graph_based_generative_recom/">SPRIG: Semantic-ID-enhanced Paths for Knowledge Graph-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将内容派生的分层语义 ID 注入知识图谱路径语言建模，以 768 个共享码替代逐物品词表，nDCG@10 较 KGGLM 提升 44%/58% 且参数与算力更低。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;注意力邻居聚合&quot;,&quot;自适应间隔 BPR&quot;,&quot;相似度辅助损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2021/graph_rec/graph_attention_collaborative_similarity_embedding_for_recommender_system/">Graph Attention Collaborative Similarity Embedding for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>注意力邻居聚合 + 用户/物品二阶相似度辅助损失 + 自适应间隔 BPR，全库排序 Recall@20 最高相对提升 6.34%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/排序&quot;,&quot;图注意力&quot;,&quot;动态静态表示&quot;,&quot;物品相关图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2022/social_group_rec/graph_neural_networks_with_dynamic_and_static_representations_for_social_re/">Graph Neural Networks with Dynamic and Static Representations for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GNN-DSR用LSTM与图注意力建模用户/物品的动态-静态表示，并聚合社交图与物品相关图，在Ciao/Epinions/Delicious上提升评分预测与物品排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;类别感知会话图&quot;,&quot;目标类别意图迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2022/sequential_rec/intention_adaptive_graph_neural_network_for_category_aware_session_based_re/">Intention Adaptive Graph Neural Network for Category-aware Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出类别感知会话推荐新任务 CSBR，构建含目标类别节点的异构会话图，用意图自适应 GAT 将与目标类别相关的历史交互迁移到目标类别域，P@20 最高提升 6.30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2022&quot;,&quot;自监督对比与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;多视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;行为区分对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2022/self_supervised_denoising/multi_view_multi_behavior_contrastive_learning_in_recommendation/">Multi-view Multi-behavior Contrastive Learning in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用行为级、视图级、行为优先级三类对比任务统一多行为多视图推荐，Tmall/CIKM 采样排序下 NDCG@10 相对提升 7.4%/6.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2023&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;可学习数据增强&quot;,&quot;对抗训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2023/self_supervised_denoising/adversarial_learning_data_augmentation_for_graph_contrastive_learning_in_re/">Adversarial Learning Data Augmentation for Graph Contrastive Learning in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 InfoMin/InfoMax 对抗框架学习可微的边增删图增强算子，四数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@10 最高相对 NCL 提升 23.5%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;反事实数据增强&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐&quot;,&quot;对比约束&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2023/graph_rec/data_augmented_counterfactual_learning_for_bundle_recommendation/">Data-Augmented Counterfactual Learning for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用启发式采样器生成反事实用户-捆绑包-物品图视图，并以自适应阈值反事实损失约束训练，缓解捆绑推荐双向稀疏，全库排序下 R@20 最高提升约 9.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 常识知识图谱&quot;,&quot;互信息最大化对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2024/graph_rec/common_sense_enhanced_knowledge_based_recommendation_with_large_language_mo/">Common Sense Enhanced Knowledge-based Recommendation with Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ChatGPT 离线生成类目互补/替代常识图，并以互信息最大化对齐硬合并失败的两张知识图，为 CKE/CFKG 带来最高 8.5% 的 nDCG 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;表征解耦&quot;,&quot;梯度反转层&quot;,&quot;跨域知识迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/a_unified_framework_for_adaptive_representation_enhancement_and_inversed_le/">A Unified Framework for Adaptive Representation Enhancement and Inversed Learning in Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>解耦共享/专属表征，用 LightGCN 域内增强 + 自注意力跨域自适应融合 + GRL 逆向约束，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 8.31%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图预训练&quot;,&quot;边信息融合&quot;,&quot;冷启动推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2025/graph_rec/genet_unleashing_the_power_of_side_information_for_recommendation_via_hyper/">GENET: Unleashing the Power of Side Information for Recommendation via Hypergraph Pre-training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将异构边信息统一构建为超图，通过超边预测与双层自监督对比预训练，再以 LightGCN/GRU 微调，Top-N 与序列推荐最高提升 38%，冷启动提升 175%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;层次表征学习&quot;,&quot;自监督学习&quot;,&quot;药物推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2026/graph_rec/self_supervised_hierarchical_representation_for_medication_recommendation/">Self-supervised Hierarchical Representation for Medication Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ICD 编码树做父节点聚合关系嵌入与码位位置编码，即插即用替换 one-hot 嵌入层，四个药物推荐基线的 Jaccard 提升 0.8%~5.7%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;符号图卷积&quot;,&quot;负偏好过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2026/graph_rec/towards_unified_modeling_for_positive_and_negative_preferences_in_sign_awar/">Towards Unified Modeling for Positive and Negative Preferences in Sign-Aware Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>单一符号图编码器统一传播正负偏好，配合负偏好过滤与多任务训练，全库排序下 Recall@10 最高提升 16.21%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/CTR&quot;,&quot;多域推荐&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;,&quot;域感知消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/exploiting_graph_structured_cross_domain_representation_for_multi_domain_re/">Exploiting Graph Structured Cross-Domain Representation for Multi-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用域感知图卷积与全局图池化建模多域交互图，CTR 负采样评测在 Amazon 2–13 域稳定超越 MDR/MTL 基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质图&quot;,&quot;Personalized PageRank&quot;,&quot;遗传算法&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/graph_rec/graph_based_recommendation_for_sparse_and_heterogeneous_user_interactions/">Graph-based Recommendation for Sparse and Heterogeneous User Interactions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用异质交互图与遗传算法学习边权，PPR 全库排序；教育集 MRR@5 提升 6.7%，小样本保险集优于 NGCF。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;归纳式推荐&quot;,&quot;物品图卷积&quot;,&quot;Top-K 剪枝&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/graph_rec/item_graph_convolution_collaborative_filtering_for_inductive_recommendation/">Item Graph Convolution Collaborative Filtering for Inductive Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出物品图卷积协同过滤 IGCCF，用余弦物品图与 top-K 剪枝学习物品嵌入、以历史交互加权构造用户嵌入，实现免重训的归纳推荐，在四个数据集上优于 LightGCN 等基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/可解释重排&quot;,&quot;路径推理&quot;,&quot;可复现性基准&quot;,&quot;推荐公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/graph_rec/knowledge_is_power_understanding_is_impact_utility_and_beyond_goals_explana/">Knowledge is Power, Understanding is Impact: Utility and Beyond Goals, Explanation Quality, and Fairness in Path Reasoning Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 PGPR/CAFE/UCPR 三篇路径推理推荐，用 16 指标统一协议揭示其以效用与覆盖度换取惊喜度、多样性与可解释保真度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;鲁棒性评估&quot;,&quot;边级扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/robustness_in_fairness_against_edge_level_perturbations_in_gnn_based_recomm/">Robustness in Fairness against Edge-level Perturbations in GNN-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>ECIR 2024 去偏公平研究首次评估图推荐系统在边级扰动下的公平性鲁棒性，设计可微扰动目标，揭示消费者侧公平性极为脆弱。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;异构知识图谱&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;地域公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/okra_an_explainable_heterogeneous_multi_stakeholder_job_recommender_system/">OKRA: an Explainable, Heterogeneous, Multi-Stakeholder Job Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>异构知识图谱+多头GATv2构建求职者/企业双侧嵌入，谐波均值融合打分，nDCG@10较六基线最高提升约18%，并揭示城乡地域偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;有向异构图&quot;,&quot;多视图嵌入传播&quot;,&quot;双成对损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2021/graph_rec/group_buying_recommendation_for_social_e_commerce/">Group-Buying Recommendation for Social E-Commerce</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建发起者/参与者/社交分享三类有向异构图，用视图内+跨视图 GCN 与双成对损失解团购推荐，采样排序下 Recall/NDCG 提升 2.69%–7.36%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;会话知识图谱预训练&quot;,&quot;用户状态表征&quot;,&quot;对话交互表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/improving_conversational_recommendation_system_by_pretraining_on_billions_s/">Improving Conversational Recommendation System by Pretraining on Billions Scale of Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 60 亿三元组会话知识图谱，用 GCN+TransE 联合预训练生成用户状态与对话交互表征，注入 DCN 做 CTR 排序，Alime 线上较 DCN 提升 4.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回排序&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;跨行为关系建模&quot;,&quot;图消息传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2021/graph_rec/multi_behavior_enhanced_recommendation_with_cross_interaction_collaborative/">Multi-Behavior Enhanced Recommendation with Cross-Interaction Collaborative Relation Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GNMR 用类型特定消息编码 + 跨行为注意力聚合建模多行为依赖，在 HR/NDCG@10 上相对最优基线提升 4.6%–27.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2021&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;后置解释层 (Post-hoc Explanation)&quot;,&quot;本体建模 (OWL/DL)&quot;,&quot;SPARQL 推理与知识图谱&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2021/agent_conversational_rec/semantic_modeling_for_food_recommendation_explanations/">Semantic Modeling for Food Recommendation Explanations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 OWL 本体 FEO 与 SPARQL 推理为食物推荐生成情境/对比/反事实解释，以 3 个能力问题做任务级评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/精排&quot;,&quot;微行为建模&quot;,&quot;多重图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2022/sequential_rec/micro_behavior_encoding_for_session_based_recommendation/">Micro-Behavior Encoding for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将会话转为带星节点的有向多重图，用GRU边特征与操作感知自注意力联合编码微行为序列/二元关系，全库排序H@10最高提升14.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;解耦表征学习&quot;,&quot;异构图表征&quot;,&quot;记忆网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2023/social_group_rec/disentangled_graph_social_recommendation/">Disentangled Graph Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以可微记忆单元把社交边、交互边、物品语义边解耦到多潜因子空间，构建异构社交 GNN，采样排序 HR@10 最高提升 4.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;动态异构图&quot;,&quot;邻域扰动&quot;,&quot;增量学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2023/graph_rec/instant_representation_learning_for_recommendation_over_large_dynamic_graph/">Instant Representation Learning for Recommendation over Large Dynamic Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SUPA 以采样-更新-传播架构替代邻居聚合，缓解动态多重异构图中的邻域扰动，配合单遍增量训练工作流 InsLearn，在六个真实数据集全库排序下 H@50 最高相对提升 54%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;过平滑缓解&quot;,&quot;层精炼 Layer Refinement&quot;,&quot;度敏感边丢弃&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2023/graph_rec/layer_refined_graph_convolutional_networks_for_recommendation/">Layer-refined Graph Convolutional Networks for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ego 层余弦相似度动态精炼每层传播表征，配合度敏感边剪枝，破解 GCN 推荐过平滑困境，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提 10.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图匹配&quot;,&quot;部分重叠跨域迁移&quot;,&quot;用户表征补全&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/neural_node_matching_for_multi_target_cross_domain_recommendation/">Neural Node Matching for Multi-Target Cross Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出节点匹配框架 NMCDR，用域内-域间全连接用户图匹配加虚拟交互补全，在 0.1% 极小重叠下 Amazon 平均提升 24.84%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Graph Information Bottleneck&quot;,&quot;Mixhop 图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/self_supervised_denoising/graph_augmentation_for_recommendation/">Graph Augmentation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 GIB 正则化的可学习图增强器生成去噪对比视图，配合 Mixhop 混阶编码器缓解过平滑，Recall@20 较 NCL 提升 5.9%–8.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;子图学习&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/graph_rec/knowledge_enhanced_recommendation_with_user_centric_subgraph_network/">Knowledge-Enhanced Recommendation with User-Centric Subgraph Network</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KUCNet 为每个用户-物品对构建U-I子图，用注意力GNN与PPR剪枝的用户中心计算图实现个性化、归纳式推荐，全库排序下Last-FM Recall@20较KGIN提升23.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异构交互建模&quot;,&quot;多头自注意力&quot;,&quot;预测上下文采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/cold_start_rec/all_in_one_heterogeneous_interaction_modeling_for_cold_start_rating_predict/">All-in-One: Heterogeneous Interaction Modeling for Cold-Start Rating Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HIRE 用纯 MHSA 在采样的预测上下文上端到端学习用户/物品/属性三层异构交互，三种冷启动场景下 Precision/NDCG/MAP 平均超 12 个基线 0.21/0.29/0.22。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;营销知识图谱构建&quot;,&quot;渐进式提示增强&quot;,&quot;教师蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/llm_rec/making_large_language_models_better_knowledge_miners_for_online_marketing_w/">Making Large Language Models Better Knowledge Miners for Online Marketing with Progressive Prompting Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用渐进式提示增强把 LLM 变成可控的营销知识图谱挖掘器，并以教师蒸馏出可部署的 LightPAIR，受众定向覆盖提升 15%–122%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;Steiner Tree&quot;,&quot;路径解释摘要&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/graph_rec/path_based_summary_explanations_for_graph_recommenders_extended_version/">Path-based summary explanations for graph recommenders (extended version)</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次提出路径解释的摘要化：用 Steiner Tree 与 Prize-Collecting Steiner Tree 将多条用户-物品解释路径压缩为连通子图，在可理解性、多样性、隐私与可扩展性上全面优于逐条路径基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超元路径&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2021/self_supervised_denoising/hyper_meta_path_contrastive_learning_for_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Hyper Meta-Path Contrastive Learning for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出超元路径/超元图显式刻画多行为依赖，用相邻超元图 InfoNCE 对比替代固定融合，Taobao Recall@5 相对提升 24.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图神经网络&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;隐私预算双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recommendation/">Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HeteDP 用「语义感知特征加噪 + DP-SGD 拓扑重构 + 双层隐私预算分配」双阶段保护异构图推荐，LP 最高相对提升 37.2%，但节点分类在强隐私下明显掉点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图核网络&quot;,&quot;地理-序列一致性学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2022/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/kernel_based_substructure_exploration_for_next_poi_recommendation/">Kernel-based Substructure Exploration for Next POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>地理消息传递图 + 随机游走图核序列图双模块，用 KL 一致性学习互蒸馏，AUC 较最优基线提升 3.3%/4.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;概率嵌入 Beta Distribution&quot;,&quot;高阶互补推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2022/graph_rec/towards_high_order_complementary_recommendation_via_logical_reasoning_netwo/">Towards High-Order Complementary Recommendation via Logical Reasoning Network</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Beta 概率嵌入与可微逻辑算子（投影/否定/交集）建模非对称互补关系，并以注意力聚合查询集合实现高阶互补推荐，Amazon 六类目 Hit@3 最高相对提升 155%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023 Workshop&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;行为数据失衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/graph_rec/alleviating_behavior_data_imbalance_for_multi_behavior_graph_collaborative_/">Alleviating Behavior Data Imbalance for Multi-Behavior Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多任务 LightGCN + 富行为表征注入稀疏行为 + 梯度路径隔离缓解点击/加购/购买数据失衡，Beibei/Taobao 上 Recall@20 最高提升 35.84%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Neural ODE&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;,&quot;连续图传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/graph_rec/graph_neural_ordinary_differential_equations_based_method_for_collaborative/">Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations-based method for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 1–2 层 GCN 参数化导数函数、以 Neural ODE 积分替代多层堆叠与层组合，Euler 求解器在四个 Amazon 数据集上取得最优 Recall/NDCG 且训练最快。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDMW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;异质图注意力&quot;,&quot;跨校选课推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/heterogeneity_aware_cross_school_electives_recommendation_a_hybrid_federate/">Heterogeneity-aware Cross-school Electives Recommendation: a Hybrid Federated Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>为跨校选课构建校内异质图 + 边感知注意力 + 约束矩阵分解联邦训练，在 1:99 采样排序下 HR@10 相对 FedGNN 提升 15.9%~23.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权&quot;,&quot;反馈回路&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2021/debias_fairness_industrial/correcting_exposure_bias_for_link_recommendation/">Correcting Exposure Bias for Link Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以倾向分重加权构造链接推荐的真风险估计器并联合训练，半合成引文数据 Precision 67.24→83.28，真风险估计 RMSE 由 1.50 降至 0.03，并抑制反馈回路与 FOS 同质化。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;属性图模式&quot;,&quot;记忆增强Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/sequential_rec/enhancing_user_intent_capture_in_session_based_recommendation_with_attribut/">Enhancing User Intent Capture in Session-Based Recommendation with Attribute Patterns</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以频繁属性图模式作记忆增强会话 Transformer，全库 top-K 评测，公开与工业数据平均提升约4.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;SVD 图增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/self_supervised_denoising/lightgcl_simple_yet_effective_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">LightGCL: Simple Yet Effective Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以截断 SVD 重构交互图生成全局协同视图，并与主视图直接对比，在五个基准的全库排序中 Recall@20 最高提升 23%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/排序学习&quot;,&quot;战略激励&quot;,&quot;多样性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/performative_recommendation_diversifying_content_via_strategic_incentives/">Performative Recommendation: Diversifying Content via Strategic Incentives</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将创作者战略响应嵌入可微排序，以多样性正则激励内容多样化；Yelp 动态重训练下同 NDCG 损失使多样性翻倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/表征学习&quot;,&quot;纵向联邦学习&quot;,&quot;隐私保护图神经网络&quot;,&quot;随机投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/vertical_federated_graph_neural_network_for_recommender_system/">Vertical Federated Graph Neural Network for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个纵向联邦 GNN 推荐框架 VerFedGNN，以随机投影传输邻居聚合矩阵、三元量化扰动公共梯度，RMSE 逼近集中式且去匿名攻击 F1 从 0.90 降至 0.02。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦图学习&quot;,&quot;负迁移抑制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/federated_graph_learning_for_cross_domain_recommendation/">Federated Graph Learning for Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FedGCDR，以 HVH 联邦图学习 + DP 知识迁移 + 虚拟用户图扩展过滤负迁移，在 Amazon 16 域采样排序上显著优于 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序（序列下一项）&quot;,&quot;图正则&quot;,&quot;嵌入优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/graph_rec/graph_enhanced_optimizers_for_structure_aware_recommendation_embedding_evol/">Graph-enhanced Optimizers for Structure-aware Recommendation Embedding Evolution</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出SEvo，将图正则注入嵌入更新而非GNN模块；Leave-One-Out全库排序下多backbone平均提升9%–23%，百万节点数据最高139%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表征增强&quot;,&quot;测试时增强&quot;,&quot;消息传递机理&quot;,&quot;轻量图协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/graph_rec/how_does_message_passing_improve_collaborative_filtering/">How Does Message Passing Improve Collaborative Filtering?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示消息传递主要靠前向邻居表征而非梯度，提出仅推理时聚合的TAG-CF，全库排序下冷/低度用户最高提升39.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLE 流形学习&quot;,&quot;EASE 闭式解&quot;,&quot;嵌入正则化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/collaborative_filtering/implicitslim_and_how_it_improves_embedding_based_collaborative_filtering/">ImplicitSLIM and How it Improves Embedding-based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 EASE 闭式解嵌入 LLE 流形学习框架，用 Woodbury 恒等式在 $L\times L$空间直接抽取嵌入，作为任意 CF 模型的初始化与正则，免去$I\times I$ 相似度矩阵存储。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;成对表征&quot;,&quot;混合双塔架构&quot;,&quot;时序异构图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/graph_rec/contextgnn_beyond_two_tower_recommendation_systems/">ContextGNN: Beyond Two-Tower Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出局部成对表征+全局双塔回退的混合GNN架构，在RelBench十个任务上平均MAP较最强成对基线提升20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Neural ODE&quot;,&quot;知识图谱偏好对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/spot_trip_dual_preference_driven_out_of_town_trip_recommendation/">SPOT-Trip: Dual-Preference Driven Out-of-Town Trip Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次形式化异地行程推荐，用知识图谱静态偏好+Neural ODE动态偏好解耦建模，F1最高提升17.01%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;互补信号&quot;,&quot;流形增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/multimodal_rec/can_complementary_signals_bridge_similarity_islands_manifold_augmented_grap/">Can Complementary Signals Bridge Similarity Islands? Manifold-Augmented Graph Embedding for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 MAGE，用离线 Soft Conceptor 残差注入 + 在线非对称双流 GNN 桥接互补品“相似性孤岛”，四个 Amazon 数据集 Full-Ranking 全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图信息瓶颈&quot;,&quot;跨行为对比学习&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/self_supervised_denoising/gcib_graph_contrastive_information_bottleneck_for_multi_behavior_recommenda/">GCIB: Graph Contrastive Information Bottleneck for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用图信息瓶颈在结构层剪枝辅助行为噪声边、用跨行为 InfoNCE 在特征层对齐目标语义，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 NDCG@10 最高提升 37.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双画像同质性门控&quot;,&quot;免训练反思更新&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/harp_training_free_dual_profile_agentic_communication_for_llm_based_recomme/">HARP: Training-Free Dual-Profile Agentic Communication for LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 推荐重构为四阶段智能体通信，用双画像、同质性门控检索与单次反思三个免训练算子补齐缺失环节，Hit@1 相对提升 6.9%–24.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;低通谱滤波&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/low_pass_personalized_subgraph_federated_recommendation/">Low-pass Personalized Subgraph Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用低通谱滤波提取跨子图稳定的结构信号做个性化联邦聚合，并以局部流行度偏差感知 margin 抑制长尾偏置，五数据集 NDCG@20 最高提升 24.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;,&quot;对比学习损失&quot;,&quot;邻居对加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/self_supervised_denoising/rethinking_contrastive_learning_for_graph_collaborative_filtering_limitatio/">Rethinking Contrastive Learning for Graph Collaborative Filtering: Limitations and a Simple Remedy</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>展开 GCF 预测为邻居对加权聚合，揭示 SSM 仅按物品侧相似度更新且忽略邻居对类型，提出类型感知 NT-SSM，NDCG@20 最高提升 21.45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2021&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图卷积网络&quot;,&quot;共享账户隐潜用户&quot;,&quot;域感知注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/cross_domain_multi_scenario/da_gcn_a_domain_aware_attentive_graph_convolution_network_for_shared_accoun/">DA-GCN: A Domain-aware Attentive Graph Convolution Network for Shared-account Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建跨域序列图并用域感知注意力 GCN 建模共享账户下 H 个隐潜用户，E 域 MRR@5 相对 PSJNet 提升 15.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与 POI 推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Seq2Graph 增广&quot;,&quot;类别感知图注意力&quot;,&quot;多任务类别预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/discovering_collaborative_signals_for_next_poi_recommendation_with_iterativ/">Discovering Collaborative Signals for Next POI Recommendation with Iterative Seq2Graph Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将孤立 POI 序列扩增为跨序列一跳图，用类别感知图注意力与位置感知注意力联合建模，并以类别预测多任务缓解稀疏，Gowalla 上 HR@5 提升 49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多兴趣表征&quot;,&quot;时间间隔建模&quot;,&quot;虚拟中心节点图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/sequential_rec/exploring_periodicity_and_interactivity_in_multi_interest_framework_for_seq/">Exploring Periodicity and Interactivity in Multi-Interest Framework for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>IJCAI 2021｜序列推荐论文：以时间间隔矩阵建模兴趣周期性、虚拟中心节点图建模物品交互性，再经自注意力抽取多兴趣向量；在 Amazon/Taobao 全库排序下 NDCG@50 最高提升约 26%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图构造与信息传播&quot;,&quot;综述与分类体系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/graph_rec/graph_learning_based_recommender_systems_a_review/">Graph Learning based Recommender Systems: A Review</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇 GLRS 系统综述：按数据形态→图结构→学习范式三层正交归类，形式化 $R=\\arg\\max_\\Theta f(M(\\Theta)|G)$，并汇总 17 个开源实现与 16 个公开数据集。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构关系学习&quot;,&quot;消息传递范式&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/graph_rec/recent_advances_in_heterogeneous_relation_learning_for_recommendation/">Recent Advances in Heterogeneous Relation Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇异构关系学习推荐综述，以「关系表征+信息融合」两问统一社交、知识图谱与多行为三条路线，并指出多模态、稀缺、时序、可解释与实时五大方向</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端多标签集合预测&quot;,&quot;分子图消息传递 MPNN&quot;,&quot;药物相互作用可控损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/graph_rec/safedrug_dual_molecular_graph_encoders_for_recommending_effective_and_safe_/">SafeDrug: Dual Molecular Graph Encoders for Recommending Effective and Safe Drug Combinations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>全局 MPNN + 局部子结构二部图双编码器刻画药物分子，配合 PID 式 DDI 可控损失，在 MIMIC-III 上 DDI 率相对降 19.43%、Jaccard 升 2.88%，参数仅 325K</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;端到端预测&quot;,&quot;对抗分布正则&quot;,&quot;记忆网络多跳读取&quot;,&quot;药物相互作用DDI&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/self_supervised_denoising/self_supervised_adversarial_distribution_regularization_for_medication_reco/">Self-Supervised Adversarial Distribution Regularization for Medication Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SARMR 从原始 EHR 中自监督拟合低 DDI 率患者表征的高斯先验，用 GAN 对抗正则患者表征分布以降低药物相互作用，MIMIC-III 上 F1 0.6608、ΔDDI -2.72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;个性化异质图&quot;,&quot;异质图池化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2021/graph_rec/user_as_graph_user_modeling_with_heterogeneous_graph_pooling_for_news_recom/">User-as-Graph: User Modeling with Heterogeneous Graph Pooling for News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户行为构造成个性化异质图，以类型感知异质图池化学习用户兴趣，在 MIND 曝光排序上稳定提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质图层次注意力&quot;,&quot;集合 Transformer&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2022/graph_rec/reciperec_a_heterogeneous_graph_learning_model_for_recipe_recommendation/">RecipeRec: A Heterogeneous Graph Learning Model for Recipe Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建用户-食谱-食材异质图 URI-Graph，用层次注意力异质 GNN、食材集合 Transformer 与图对比增强联合建模内容与高阶协同信号，Leave-One-Out 采样排序下 HR@10 达 45.70。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;意图解耦表征&quot;,&quot;意图级对比学习&quot;,&quot;码率约简正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2023/graph_rec/intent_aware_recommendation_via_disentangled_graph_contrastive_learning/">Intent-aware Recommendation via Disentangled Graph Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以用户-物品-概念图为基础，用概念软聚类语义基引导行为意图解耦，配合意图级对比学习与码率约简正则，在 ML-100k/ML-1M/MtBusiness 全库排序下 Recall@20 最高相对提升 8.52%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;过平滑 (Over-smoothing)&quot;,&quot;模型无关即插即用框架&quot;,&quot;协同邻域校正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2024/graph_rec/dgr_a_general_graph_desmoothing_framework_for_recommendation_via_global_and/">DGR: A General Graph Desmoothing Framework for Recommendation via Global and Local Perspectives</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以全局向量扰动 (GMP) 与局部协同校正损失 (LEC) 构成模型无关的过平滑抑制框架，即插即用于 5 种 GCN 骨干，在 5 个基准上 Full-Ranking 平均 Recall@20 提升 2%~11%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图后门攻击&quot;,&quot;单节点触发器&quot;,&quot;LightGCN 鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/single_node_trigger_backdoor_attacks_in_graph_based_recommendation_systems/">Single-Node Trigger Backdoor Attacks in Graph-Based Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出单节点触发器后门攻击 SNT-BA，仅注入 1 个虚假用户即可让目标物品在 99% 目标用户 Top-10 中曝光，同时 HR@20 仅降约 5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;学习路径分段漂移&quot;,&quot;语义-结构对齐融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/llm_rec/llm_enhanced_knowledge_and_learning_path_understanding_for_graph_based_educ/">LLM-Enhanced Knowledge and Learning Path Understanding for Graph-based Educational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 摘要资源知识与学习路径分段模式，双分支融合图结构表征，全库排序下 Recall@10 最高提升 7.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2026&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;上下文老虎机&quot;,&quot;知识追踪&quot;,&quot;课程推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/rl_bandit_rec/pace_a_personalized_adaptive_curriculum_engine_for_9_1_1_call_taker_trainin/">PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用技能图信念传播+个体学习/遗忘动力学+Thompson Sampling 上下文老虎机为 9-1-1 调度员推荐训练场景，Z2H 缩短 19.50%、终局掌握度提升 10.95%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;软情感指派&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比对齐&quot;,&quot;情感交换机制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/graph_rec/sentiment_aware_rating_based_recommendation_via_semantic_enhanced_item_alig/">Sentiment-aware Rating-based Recommendation via Semantic-enhanced Item Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用经验贝叶斯软情感指派替代硬阈值，配合协同-语义图与锚引导单向对齐，以及显式正负情感对冲打分，在四个 Amazon 5-core 数据集上全库排序取得 MSE 23.08%、NDCG@10 21.51% 的提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多教师在线蒸馏&quot;,&quot;模态不平衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/multimodal_rec/three_minds_one_student_online_multi_teacher_knowledge_distillation_for_mul/">Three Minds, One Student: Online Multi-Teacher Knowledge Distillation for Multimodal Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多模态推荐重构为“三教师-单学生”在线蒸馏，在排序级与表征级选择性迁移 ID/图/文偏好，Full-Ranking 上最高相对提升 7.22%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图神经网络Embedding增强&quot;,&quot;特征稀疏&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/dual_graph_enhanced_embedding_neural_network_for_ctr_prediction/">Dual Graph enhanced Embedding Neural Network for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用属性图+协同图双图卷积精炼Embedding，以分治式字段卷积与课程学习式组织学习缓解特征/行为稀疏，Alimama AUC 提升 8.83%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;关系拓扑保持&quot;,&quot;模型压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2021/collaborative_filtering/topology_distillation_for_recommender_system/">Topology Distillation for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将教师表示空间的关系拓扑分层（组级原型+组内实体级）蒸馏给轻量学生，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@50 较 SOTA DE 提升 3.0–7.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;捆绑包推荐&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/self_supervised_denoising/crosscbr_cross_view_contrastive_learning_for_bundle_recommendation/">CrossCBR: Cross-view Contrastive Learning for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KDD 2022][自监督对比学习与交互去噪] 以 LightGCN 分别学习捆绑包视图与物品视图，用跨视图 InfoNCE 对齐双视图并增强自判别性，三个数据集 Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@20 最高相对提升 29.6%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何&quot;,&quot;洛伦兹模型&quot;,&quot;间隔排序损失&quot;,&quot;信息负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/collaborative_filtering/hicf_hyperbolic_informative_collaborative_filtering/">HICF: Hyperbolic Informative Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>拆解双曲与欧氏 CF 在头/尾物品上的行为差异，用几何感知自适应 margin 与双曲信息负采样改造间隔排序损失，全库指标最高提升 12.92%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;二值量化&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/collaborative_filtering/learning_binarized_graph_representations_with_multi_faceted_quantization_re/">Learning Binarized Graph Representations with Multi-faceted Quantization Reinforcement for Top-K Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>BiGeaR 以逐层二值化、Top-R 推理蒸馏和 Dirac 梯度估计，在5个基准上接近全精度 LightGCN 且约8倍加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为序列建模&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;低秩自注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/sequential_rec/multi_behavior_hypergraph_enhanced_transformer_for_sequential_recommendatio/">Multi-Behavior Hypergraph-Enhanced Transformer for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多尺度低秩 Transformer 捕获行为感知短期转移，超图网络建模跨行为长程依赖，Leave-One-Out+1:100 采样排序下 HR@5 最高提升 48.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图 Transformer&quot;,&quot;自监督去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/graph_rec/self_supervised_hypergraph_transformer_for_recommender_systems/">Self-Supervised Hypergraph Transformer for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 K 个可学习隐超边 + 线性注意力 Transformer 建模全局协同关系，并以元网络标注边可靠度做局部-全局自增广，全库排序 Recall@20 最高提升 44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自适应视图生成&quot;,&quot;图边去噪&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/self_supervised_denoising/adaptive_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">Adaptive Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 VGAE 生成视图与可学习边去噪视图替代手工图增广，为 LightGCN 提供自适应对比信号，全库排序下 Recall@20 相对提升约 5–8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;,&quot;多准则推荐&quot;,&quot;PairNorm&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/graph_rec/criteria_tell_you_more_than_ratings_criteria_preference_aware_light_graph_c/">Criteria Tell You More than Ratings: Criteria Preference-Aware Light Graph Convolution for Effective Multi-Criteria Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多准则评分展开为准则-物品扩展二部图，用轻量图卷积 + 用户准则偏好/物品准则双嵌入建模，全库排序下 P@5 最高提升 141.2%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/模型设计&quot;,&quot;联合搜索&quot;,&quot;AutoML&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/collaborative_filtering/efficient_and_joint_hyperparameter_and_architecture_search_for_collaborativ/">Efficient and Joint Hyperparameter and Architecture Search for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出CF超参与架构联合搜索：超参筛减+频率子图两阶段BORE/RF搜索，全库Recall@20最高提升13.02%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;有效电导&quot;,&quot;难负样本采样&quot;,&quot;数据子采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/graph_rec/graph_based_model_agnostic_data_subsampling_for_recommendation_systems/">Graph-Based Model-Agnostic Data Subsampling for Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户-物品二部图的有效电导度量负样本难度并做边传播平滑，实现免 pilot 模型的数据子采样，KuaiRec/MIND 上 AUC 稳定超越均匀采样且可与模型法互补融合。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱合理化&quot;,&quot;掩码自编码器&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/graph_rec/knowledge_graph_self_supervised_rationalization_for_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Self-Supervised Rationalization for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KGRec 用注意力合理化分数驱动掩码重建生成式任务与去噪跨视图对比学习，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 较 SOTA 提升 2.3%–4.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双层级三元图&quot;,&quot;时段分解与时间门控&quot;,&quot;外卖推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/sequential_rec/modeling_dual_period_varying_preferences_for_takeaway_recommendation/">Modeling Dual Period-Varying Preferences for Takeaway Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将外卖用户-商家-菜品三元图按时段硬切分为双层级周期子图，配合用户感知与时间感知软门控融合，采样排序下 Hit@10 相对提升 3.6–7.3%，线上 GMV +0.70%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;三部图元路径&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/graph_rec/consistency_and_discrepancy_based_contrastive_tripartite_graph_learning_for/">Consistency and Discrepancy-Based Contrastive Tripartite Graph Learning for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可达/不可达元路径导出一致性与差异性系数，经 GCN 极限理论免训练预计算，配合 CD 对比损失解决三部图推荐极端冷启动。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;用户关系图&quot;,&quot;个性化物品嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/gpfedrec_graph_guided_personalization_for_federated_recommendation/">GPFedRec: Graph-guided Personalization for Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>服务端用客户端物品嵌入构建用户关系图引导联邦聚合，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@10 最高相对提升 3.38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表征学习&quot;,&quot;信息瓶颈&quot;,&quot;社交图去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/social_group_rec/graph_bottlenecked_social_recommendation/">Graph Bottlenecked Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用信息瓶颈原理学习去噪社交图：偏好引导的可微边丢弃 + HSIC 瓶颈正则，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@20 最高相对提升 17.06%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱图滤波&quot;,&quot;广义图归一化&quot;,&quot;免训练协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/graph_rec/how_powerful_is_graph_filtering_for_recommendation/">How Powerful is Graph Filtering for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以谱锐度视角提出广义图归一化 G²N 与个性化图滤波 IGF，免训练闭式 SGFCF 在四个密度差异数据集上 nDCG@10 最高提升 14.3%，训练耗时降低三个数量级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;偏序推荐图&quot;,&quot;多行为协同过滤&quot;,&quot;偏序 BPR 采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/graph_rec/multi_behavior_collaborative_filtering_with_partial_order_graph_convolution/">Multi-Behavior Collaborative Filtering with Partial Order Graph Convolutional Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以分级偏序秩函数融合多行为图为单张加权 POG，POGCN 图卷积 + 偏序 BPR 采样，全库排序下三基准 Mean NDCG 提升 19.67%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;健康行为干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/graph_rec/nudgerank_digital_algorithmic_nudging_for_personalized_health/">NudgeRank: Digital Algorithmic Nudging for Personalized Health</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以可扩展异构知识图谱+注意力图卷积实现百万级人群个性化健康提醒，离线Precision@3提升45%，线上日步数+6.17%、MVPA+7.61%。</p>
</dd>
