<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;决策边界感知扰动&quot;,&quot;对抗图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/self_supervised_denoising/towards_robust_recommendation_via_decision_boundary_aware_graph_contrastive/">Towards Robust Recommendation via Decision Boundary-aware Graph Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>决策边界感知扰动、关系感知对抗图对比与间隔最大化；五数据集全库排序最高相对提升 14.14%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双意图建模&quot;,&quot;图传播与嵌入翻译&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/unified_dual_intent_translation_for_joint_modeling_of_search_and_recommenda/">Unified Dual-Intent Translation for Joint Modeling of Search and Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用搜索查询监督推荐侧需求意图生成，并以双意图翻译传播统一建模搜索与推荐，离线 Hit@5 约 +4.8%，线上 GMV +1.46%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Box Embedding&quot;,&quot;标签感知推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/graph_rec/when_box_meets_graph_neural_network_in_tag_aware_recommendation/">When Box Meets Graph Neural Network in Tag-aware Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户/物品/标签统一建模为超矩形盒，用交并逻辑算子替代 GNN 均值聚合，并以 Gumbel 平滑盒体积作为匹配分，全库排序下全面超越 LFGCF。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 自动构图&quot;,&quot;残差向量量化&quot;,&quot;元路径消息传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/graph_rec/an_automatic_graph_construction_framework_based_on_large_language_models_fo/">An Automatic Graph Construction Framework based on Large Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 逐点生成语义向量 + 残差量化抽取隐因子作额外节点，O(N) 构图并设计元路径聚合，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@10 最高提升 43.57%，华为广告 A/B 提升 RPM 2.69%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;频谱偏移校正&quot;,&quot;图结构化辅助信息&quot;,&quot;多模态/社交推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/graph_rec/collaborative_filtering_meets_spectrum_shift_connecting_user_item_interacti/">Collaborative Filtering Meets Spectrum Shift: Connecting User-Item Interaction with Graph-Structured Side Information</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示增广图频谱右移现象，提出仅含平移/缩放两因子的 SSC 校正，即插即用提升 LightGCN/JGCF 最高 23%，零额外开销。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;组合优化约束&quot;,&quot;负迁移抑制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/multi_task_reranking/combinatorial_optimization_perspective_based_framework_for_multi_behavior_r/">Combinatorial Optimization Perspective based Framework for Multi-behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多行为融合视为组合优化问题施加三阶段约束，并用分布拟合多专家网络抑制负迁移，三数据集 HR@10 最高提升 49.91%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;离散码向量量化&quot;,&quot;虚拟邻居增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/self_supervised_denoising/enhancing_graph_contrastive_learning_with_reliable_and_informative_augmenta/">Enhancing Graph Contrastive Learning with Reliable and Informative Augmentation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用端到端多级向量量化学习离散码，将码作为虚拟邻居与语义正样本构建三重对比视图，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@10 最高提升 17.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布式图学习&quot;,&quot;子图采样表格化&quot;,&quot;工业级 GNN 系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/gigl_large_scale_graph_neural_networks_at_snapchat/">GiGL: Large-Scale Graph Neural Networks at Snapchat</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>Snapchat 开源十亿级图学习库 GiGL，以表格化子图采样+PyG 解耦实现 900M 节点/16.8B 边图 12 小时内端到端训练推理，支撑 35+ 线上发布。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;轻量化GNN&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/graph_rec/lightkg_efficient_knowledge_aware_recommendations_with_simplified_gnn_archi/">LightKG: Efficient Knowledge-Aware Recommendations with Simplified GNN Architecture</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用标量对编码关系+线性聚合+免子图对比层，在稀疏场景超越12个KGRS基线5.8%并省84.3%训练时间</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Pareto 多目标优化&quot;,&quot;符号图结构学习&quot;,&quot;LLM 知识注入解释&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/multi_task_reranking/mopi_hfrs_a_multi_objective_personalized_health_aware_food_recommendation_s/">MOPI-HFRS: A Multi-objective Personalized Health-aware Food Recommendation System with LLM-enhanced Interpretation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建首个融合 NHANES 医疗数据的健康感知食物推荐基准，用符号图结构学习 + Pareto 多目标优化平衡偏好/个性化健康/营养多样性，并以知识注入 LLM 生成可解释理由。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为序列建模&quot;,&quot;交互级依赖图&quot;,&quot;多粒度注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/sequential_rec/multi_grained_preference_enhanced_transformer_for_multi_behavior_sequential/">Multi-Grained Preference Enhanced Transformer for Multi-Behavior Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>M-GPT 用交互级图卷积建模跨类型行为依赖，并以多粒度多尺度注意力抽取行为感知偏好，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@5 相对提升 12.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图表征&quot;,&quot;多任务对比学习&quot;,&quot;大规模图引擎&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/self_supervised_denoising/omnisage_large_scale_multi_entity_heterogeneous_graph_representation_learni/">OmniSage: Large Scale, Multi-Entity Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一异构图 GNN、内容特征与用户序列的多任务对比学习框架，支撑 Pinterest 十亿级实体通用表征，线上全站 Repin 提升约 2.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图神经ODE&quot;,&quot;时间引导扩散&quot;,&quot;物品演化图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/sequential_rec/time_matters_enhancing_sequential_recommendations_with_time_guided_graph_ne/">Time Matters: Enhancing Sequential Recommendations with Time-Guided Graph Neural ODEs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用时间引导扩散补全稀疏用户时间图、图神经ODE对齐用户兴趣与物品演化，五数据集Recall@20最高提升46%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;方面解耦&quot;,&quot;多重图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/copersona_collaborative_persona_graphs_for_robust_llm_personalization/">CoPersona: Collaborative Persona Graphs for Robust LLM Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用方面级多重人物图+双分支推理补全稀疏用户画像，Amazon六域生成式评测全面超越DEP/DPL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;特征频域谱滤波&quot;,&quot;可靠性感知融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/self_supervised_denoising/dynamic_spectral_denoising_with_global_context_attention_for_multi_behavior/">Dynamic Spectral Denoising with Global-Context Attention for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>沿特征维做 RFFT 学习视图自适应谱调制净化表征，再以净化全局锚做可靠性感知注意力融合，全库排序下 Taobao HR@10 相对提升 26.17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义画像生成&quot;,&quot;知识图谱传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/enriching_semantic_profiles_into_knowledge_graph_for_recommender_systems_us/">Enriching Semantic Profiles into Knowledge Graph for Recommender Systems Using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 为 KG 全实体生成语义画像，以可逆加性注入+成对匹配对齐，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@40 最高提升 2.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱滤波&quot;,&quot;频率信号缩放&quot;,&quot;空间翻转&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/graph_rec/how_do_graph_signals_affect_recommendation_unveiling_the_mystery_of_low_and/">How Do Graph Signals Affect Recommendation: Unveiling the Mystery of Low and High-Frequency Graph Signals</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论证明同绝对值低频/高频图信号在推荐中等价，提出频率信号缩放器与空间翻转插件，在四个数据集全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升约 3.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;宏图专家&quot;,&quot;多任务图合并&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/multi_task_reranking/macro_graph_of_experts_for_billion_scale_multi_task_recommendation/">Macro Graph of Experts for Billion-Scale Multi-Task Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以MTMG合并多任务宏图，结合宏图专家与级联读出；Taobao平均AUC提升13.42%，线上GMV较MMoE提升16.46%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;KG-RAG&quot;,&quot;MoE&quot;,&quot;多智能体强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/mixture_of_experts_knowledge_graph_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_multi/">Mixture-of-Experts Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Agent LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MoE 多粒度 KG 检索智能体按查询复杂度路由检索粒度，配合知识对齐与对比偏好优化，在 20 候选池协议下 Accuracy 相对提升 5.8%–20.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐与离散语义ID&quot;,&quot;召回/端到端&quot;,&quot;扩散推荐&quot;,&quot;硬负采样&quot;,&quot;自由能最大化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/generative_rec/towards_a_tri_view_diffusion_framework_for_recommendation/">Towards A Tri-View Diffusion Framework for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从热力学视角用 Helmholtz 自由能统一扩散推荐的“能量最大化”与经典模型的“熵最小化”，配合各向异性去噪器与接受-拒绝 Gumbel 硬负采样，全库排序下平均超越 DiffRec 约 12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2021&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;引力启发图自编码器&quot;,&quot;有向链接预测&quot;,&quot;工业音乐推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2021/cold_start_rec/cold_start_similar_artists_ranking_with_gravity_inspired_graph_autoencoders/">Cold Start Similar Artists Ranking with Gravity-Inspired Graph Autoencoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把新艺人相似艺人排序建模为有向属性图链接预测，用引力启发图自编码器的质量-距离解码器，全库排序下 NDCG@200 相对提升 21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;稀疏特征分解&quot;,&quot;信息增益赋权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2021/graph_rec/sparse_feature_factorization_for_recommender_systems_with_knowledge_graphs/">Sparse Feature Factorization for Recommender Systems with Knowledge Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KGFlex 以熵驱动信息增益为用户-特征对赋权，仅在用户相关 KG 特征上做稀疏因子分解，全库排序下精度可比、多样性与长尾覆盖显著更优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回（KGNN 训练加速）&quot;,&quot;激活量化&quot;,&quot;随机舍入&quot;,&quot;显存压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/graph_rec/tinykg_memory_efficient_training_framework_for_knowledge_graph_neural_recom/">TinyKG: Memory-Efficient Training Framework for Knowledge Graph Neural Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用均匀量化+随机舍入把 KGNN 前向激活压缩为 INT2 存显存，反向反量化算梯度，激活内存降 7× 而 Recall@20 仅降约 1.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;全局新闻图&quot;,&quot;GGNN&quot;,&quot;实体图增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/graph_rec/going_beyond_local_global_graph_enhanced_personalized_news_recommendations/">Going Beyond Local: Global Graph-Enhanced Personalized News Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用跨用户全局新闻图(GGNN)与全局实体图增强新闻/候选表示，MIND-large AUC 69.54、nDCG@10 44.19，兼顾多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;低比特量化&quot;,&quot;梯度直通估计器&quot;,&quot;Hessian 迹估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/graph_rec/hessian_aware_quantized_node_embeddings_for_recommendation/">Hessian-aware Quantized Node Embeddings for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用二阶 Hessian 迹修正 STE 的梯度失配，实现任意比特 GNN 节点量化嵌入，1-bit 下 Recall@50 较 HashGNN 平均提升约 16%，推理加速 3.6×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;混合曲率流形&quot;,&quot;几何感知 Margin&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/graph_rec/knowledge_based_multiple_adaptive_spaces_fusion_for_recommendation/">Knowledge-based Multiple Adaptive Spaces Fusion for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习曲率的统一空间兼容双曲/欧氏/球面并注意力融合多子空间，配合几何感知 margin，在三个 KG 数据集采样排序下 HR@20 最高提升 1.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;物品-属性图&quot;,&quot;多任务预训练&quot;,&quot;对齐-均匀性损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/cold_start_rec/multi_task_item_attribute_graph_pre_training_for_strict_cold_start_item_rec/">Multi-task Item-attribute Graph Pre-training for Strict Cold-start Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>抽取细粒度物品属性构建物品-属性图，以三任务对齐-均匀性预训练实现严格冷启动物品全排序召回，较 UniSRec 最高提升 64%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;截断SVD&quot;,&quot;谱聚类与社区检测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/collaborative_filtering/of_spiky_svds_and_music_recommendation/">Of Spiky SVDs and Music Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示截断 SVD 音乐嵌入空间中普遍存在的“尖峰”几何，证明其等价于变度社区（DCBM），并给出余弦/点积选择与嵌入稳定性评估的工业实践。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐与内容理解&quot;,&quot;召回/端到端&quot;,&quot;多路 Transformer&quot;,&quot;统一图神经网络融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/multimodal_rec/a_unified_graph_transformer_for_overcoming_isolations_in_multi_modal_recomm/">A Unified Graph Transformer for Overcoming Isolations in Multi-modal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>UGT 用多路 Transformer 替代预训练 CNN/Sentence-Transformer 抽取对齐图文特征，并以统一 GNN 对多模态与 ID 传播做联合融合；在 Amazon 三数据集 8:1:1 划分下 Recall@20 相对最强基线最高提升 13.97%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信隐私&quot;,&quot;解释层/决策支持&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方推荐&quot;,&quot;职业知识图谱&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/explainable_multi_stakeholder_job_recommender_systems/">Explainable Multi-Stakeholder Job Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>招聘推荐三方利益相关方解释偏好研究 + 职业知识图谱注意力 GNN（OKRA），发现用户无法区分真实/随机解释，主张转向决策支持式解释。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;后处理(图数据增广)&quot;,&quot;消费者公平性 (Consumer Fairness)&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤增广&quot;,&quot;采样策略&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/fair_augmentation_for_graph_collaborative_filtering/">Fair Augmentation for Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现并形式化“公平图增广”后处理范式，推导 3 类新采样策略，在 5 数据集×16 模型上揭示大图高效用模型有效、公平知识不可迁移。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;信息瓶颈&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;图去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/hierarchical_graph_information_bottleneck_for_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Hierarchical Graph Information Bottleneck for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用信息瓶颈原理重构多行为层次推荐，以 InfoNCE 保真、HSIC 压缩、可学习边丢弃去噪，全库排序下 HR@10 最高提升 15.08%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;邻接层对比视图&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/self_supervised_denoising/nlgcl_naturally_existing_neighbor_layers_graph_contrastive_learning_for_rec/">NLGCL: Naturally Existing Neighbor Layers Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 GNN 相邻层天然异构节点作正样本对替代图增广，免视图构建，全库排序下 NDCG@10 最高提升 12.41% 且训练最快。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;测试时增强&quot;,&quot;非参数图卷积&quot;,&quot;CTR 精排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multi_task_reranking/non_parametric_graph_convolution_for_re_ranking_in_recommendation_systems/">Non-parametric Graph Convolution for Re-ranking in Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将图卷积从训练期搬到测试期，用相似用户/物品构造增强对并加权聚合打分，重排平均提升 8.1% 而额外开销仅 0.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图神经网络&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;推荐基础模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/rankgraph_unified_heterogeneous_graph_learning_for_cross_domain_recommendat/">RankGraph: Unified Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建跨产品异构异质图，用 GPU 加速 RGCN + 对比学习产出图 token 注入推荐基础模型，线上 CTR +0.92%、CVR +2.82%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Ricci 曲率&quot;,&quot;根因归因&quot;,&quot;金融图推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/ricciflowrec_a_geometric_root_cause_recommender_using_ricci_curvature_on_fi/">RicciFlowRec: A Geometric Root Cause Recommender Using Ricci Curvature on Financial Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Ollivier/Forman-Ricci 曲率与 Ricci 流在动态金融图上做根因归因，以曲率风险惩罚重排资产，NDCG@10 提升 4.9%、Top-10 波动降 28%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;统一损失&quot;,&quot;免增广训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/self_supervised_denoising/sgcl_unifying_self_supervised_and_supervised_learning_for_graph_recommendat/">SGCL: Unifying Self-Supervised and Supervised Learning for Graph Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 BPR 与 InfoNCE 合并为单一监督图对比损失，免增广免负采样，精度与训练效率双超 SGL/SimGCL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;位置偏差控制函数&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/unbiased_recommender_systems_with_implicit_feedback/">Unbiased Recommender Systems with Implicit Feedback</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>博士论文愿景：以控制函数法纠偏排序位置偏差、以事后投影与消息传递加权纠偏 GNN 协同过滤的流行度偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回/相关性粗排&quot;,&quot;行为图增强&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏长尾&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/adsgnn_behavior_graph_augmented_relevance_modeling_in_sponsored_search/">AdsGNN: Behavior-Graph Augmented Relevance Modeling in Sponsored Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以点击二部图作互补信号，在节点/边/Token 三级融合语义与行为图，配合领域预训练与长尾蒸馏，ROC-AUC 0.881、线上 RPM +1.13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;一致性邻居采样&quot;,&quot;关系注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/social_group_rec/consisrec_enhancing_gnn_for_social_recommendation_via_consistent_neighbor_a/">ConsisRec: Enhancing GNN for Social Recommendation via Consistent Neighbor Aggregation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出一致性查询+邻居采样+关系注意力，抑制社交图中上下文级与关系级不一致噪声，在 Ciao/Epinions 评分预测上 RMSE 相对降 2.79%/1.74%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为建模&quot;,&quot;元学习&quot;,&quot;行为依赖迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/graph_rec/graph_meta_network_for_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Graph Meta Network for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用元学习为每个用户动态生成行为异质变换与预测层参数，联合建模多行为图传播与跨类型依赖；Leave-One-Out + 1:99 采样排序下，Taobao HR@10 相对 NGCF_M 提升 31.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图注意力网络&quot;,&quot;元学习嵌入生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/cold_start_rec/learning_graph_meta_embeddings_for_cold_start_ads_in_click_through_rate_pre/">Learning Graph Meta Embeddings for Cold-Start Ads in Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用广告属性图 + GAT + MAML 式元学习为新广告 ID 生成初始嵌入，在三个真实数据集上对五种 CTR 主干模型实现冷启动与预热双阶段 AUC 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;澄清问题生成&quot;,&quot;知识图谱关系注意力&quot;,&quot;TransH 偏好翻译&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/agent_conversational_rec/learning_to_ask_appropriate_questions_in_conversational_recommendation/">Learning to Ask Appropriate Questions in Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KBQG 用 KG 关系注意力引导澄清问题生成，简化动作空间后在两数据集上 SR 相对 CRM 提升 7.2%–12.9% 且显著缩短对话轮次</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;属性图匹配&quot;,&quot;内外交互解耦&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/collaborative_filtering/neural_graph_matching_based_collaborative_filtering/">Neural Graph Matching based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将属性交互解耦为内交互与交叉交互，用双属性图匹配结构分别做特征学习与偏好匹配，AUC/NDCG@10 最高提升 4.3%/16.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;二跳路径聚合&quot;,&quot;多兴趣建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/collaborative_filtering/path_based_deep_network_for_candidate_item_matching_in_recommenders/">Path-based Deep Network for Candidate Item Matching in Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 TrigNet×SimNet 对用户-物品二跳路径逐条打分并聚合召回，兼顾个性化与兴趣多样性，淘宝线上 PCTR +18.04%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Graph&quot;,&quot;Co-Attention&quot;,&quot;Interactive Matching&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/graph_rec/personalized_news_recommendation_with_knowledge_aware_interactive_matching/">Personalized News Recommendation with Knowledge-aware Interactive Matching</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KIM 用知识协同编码器与语义协同编码器实现候选新闻与用户兴趣的双向交互匹配，在 MIND/Feeds 上 nDCG@5 相对提升约 5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;异质图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/cold_start_rec/privileged_graph_distillation_for_cold_start_recommendation/">Privileged Graph Distillation for Cold Start Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将属性作为节点构建异质图教师模型，用二阶间接链接构造无 CF 边的学生图，通过特权图蒸馏实现新用户/新物品/双冷启动统一推荐，Yelp/XING/Amazon 全库排序 HR/NDCG 平均最高提升 27.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;长尾去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/self_supervised_denoising/self_supervised_graph_learning_for_recommendation/">Self-supervised Graph Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 LightGCN 上叠加节点自判别对比任务，用节点/边丢弃与随机游走构造多视图，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 9.13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;兴趣图构建&quot;,&quot;动态图池化&quot;,&quot;AUGRU&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/sequential_rec/sequential_recommendation_with_graph_neural_networks/">Sequential Recommendation with Graph Neural Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将松散物品序列用度量学习重构为兴趣图，经簇/查询双感知图卷积与动态图池化提炼激活核心兴趣，长序列场景 AUC 提升约 4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;假阴性去偏&quot;,&quot;拓扑相似度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/self_supervised_denoising/topology_aware_debiased_self_supervised_graph_learning_for_recommendation/">Topology-aware Debiased Self-supervised Graph Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用交互共现拓扑筛选假阴性并转为辅助正样本，在三个数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高相对提升约 3.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱嵌入&quot;,&quot;度量学习&quot;,&quot;共现关系建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2021/graph_rec/ugrec_modeling_directed_and_undirected_relations_for_recommendation/">UGRec: Modeling Directed and Undirected Relations for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将知识图谱有向关系与物品共现无向关系统一建模：有向关系用 TransD 关系空间投影+头尾关系感知注意力，无向关系用关系专属超平面投影最小化距离，Amazon 三数据集 Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@20 相对 SOTA 提升 3.7%–5.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;边特征图卷积&quot;,&quot;评论对齐对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/self_supervised_denoising/a_review_aware_graph_contrastive_learning_framework_for_recommendation/">A Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning Framework for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将评分+评论构建为边特征增强的用户-物品图，用评论调制邻居权重，并以节点/边判别双对比任务提供自监督信号；在5个5-core数据集上随机划分、MSE指标取得最高4.2%相对提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回与表征学习&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;表示均匀性&quot;,&quot;噪声增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/self_supervised_denoising/are_graph_augmentations_necessary_simple_graph_contrastive_learning_for_rec/">Are Graph Augmentations Necessary? Simple Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>实验揭示InfoNCE带来的表示均匀性而非图增强是增益关键，提出嵌入空间加均匀噪声的SimGCL；三数据集全库排序下Recall@20较LightGCN最高提升25.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多关系图卷积&quot;,&quot;耦合RNN时序互影响&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/empowering_next_poi_recommendation_with_multi_relational_modeling/">Empowering Next POI Recommendation with Multi-Relational Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MEMO 用多关系 GCN + 关系专属自注意力聚合异构社交图，并以耦合 RNN 建模用户-POI 时序互影响；在三个 LBSN 数据集 Recall@10 相对 STAN 提升约 2.7%–3.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;轨迹流图&quot;,&quot;Graph Enhanced Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/getnext_trajectory_flow_map_enhanced_transformer_for_next_poi_recommendatio/">GETNext: Trajectory Flow Map Enhanced Transformer for Next POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建用户无关的全局轨迹流图并用 GCN+转移动注意力图显式编码群体转移模式，融合 Transformer 序列建模，在 NYC/TKY/CA 全库排序上 Acc@5 提升约 11%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图趋势滤波&quot;,&quot;自适应平滑&quot;,&quot;鲁棒协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/graph_trend_filtering_networks_for_recommendations/">Graph Trend Filtering Networks for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 L1 图趋势滤波替换拉普拉斯等权平滑，得到边级自适应加权的嵌入传播，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@20 最高相对提升 12.77%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何&quot;,&quot;知识图谱聚合&quot;,&quot;门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/hakg_hierarchy_aware_knowledge_gated_network_for_recommendation/">HAKG: Hierarchy-Aware Knowledge Gated Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户-物品图与知识图谱同时嵌入双曲空间，用双曲关系传递聚合、层级锥角约束与双物品嵌入门控融合，Full-Ranking 下 nDCG@20 最高提升 15.43%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;属性树聚合&quot;,&quot;层次意图建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/hien_hierarchical_intention_embedding_network_for_click_through_rate_predic/">HIEN: Hierarchical Intention Embedding Network for Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用属性树自底向上聚合 + 层次注意力双向意图建模精修Embedding，作为即插即用输入模块，在阿里/Tmall/腾讯三个数据集上AUC较DG-ENN最高提升0.86%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;过平滑缓解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/hypergraph_contrastive_collaborative_filtering/">Hypergraph Contrastive Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>参数化低秩超图学习刻画全局依赖 + 局部-全局跨视图 InfoNCE，缓解 GNN 过平滑与交互稀疏，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@20 相对 LightGCN 提升 24.7%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;归一化系数 r&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/investigating_accuracy_novelty_performance_for_graph_based_collaborative_fi/">Investigating Accuracy-Novelty Performance for Graph-based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论证明 GNN 对称归一化是流行度偏置之源，提出零开销插件 r-AdjNorm，通过单一系数 r 控制归一化强度，在三个基准上不损精度的同时显著提升新颖性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱去噪&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;图增广&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/knowledge_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以KG结构一致性为去噪信号指导交互图增广，跨视图对比学习在三个数据集全库排序下Recall@20 提升约4-6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱图滤波&quot;,&quot;图去噪编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/less_is_more_reweighting_important_spectral_graph_features_for_recommendati/">Less is More: Reweighting Important Spectral Graph Features for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>谱域分析发现仅少数平滑/粗糙图特征有效，用指数核带通滤波的 GDE 替代邻域聚合，5 数据集全库排序最优且比 LightGCN 快 12 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;kNN 物品语义图&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/multi_level_cross_view_contrastive_learning_for_knowledge_aware_recommender/">Multi-level Cross-view Contrastive Learning for Knowledge-aware Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>拆解用户-物品-实体图为协同/语义/结构三视图，做局部-全局跨视图对比学习，AUC 较 KGIN 提升 1.6–2.8 个百分点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异构信息网络&quot;,&quot;Graph-Masked Transformer&quot;,&quot;一致性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/neighbour_interaction_based_click_through_rate_prediction_via_graph_masked_/">Neighbour Interaction based Click-Through Rate Prediction via Graph-masked Transformer</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 CTR 重建为异构信息图上 user-item 局部邻域连边预测，用四类交互图 + 头分组图掩码 Transformer 编码邻域，微信线上 CTR +21.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质超图&quot;,&quot;价格偏好建模&quot;,&quot;协同引导学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/sequential_rec/price_does_matter_modeling_price_and_interest_preferences_in_session_based_/">Price DOES Matter! Modeling Price and Interest Preferences in Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将价格离散为等概率价格等级并构建异质超图，用双通道聚合+协同引导门控联合建模价格与兴趣偏好，全库排序下 Prec@20 最高提升 6.86%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/端到端排序&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;协同注意力&quot;,&quot;包围子图与门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/graph_rec/unify_local_and_global_information_for_top_n_recommendation/">Unify Local and Global Information for Top-$N$ Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双分支 KADM：协同注意力编码局部交互、关系感知 GNN 编码包围子图，门控融合后在 1:100 采样排序下 Recall@20 提升 3.1%~7.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;方向性扩散噪声&quot;,&quot;线性图 Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/self_supervised_denoising/a_directional_diffusion_graph_transformer_for_recommendation/">A Directional Diffusion Graph Transformer for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以各向异性方向高斯噪声替代等方向噪声做嵌入级扩散，并用条件线性 Transformer 反向去噪，全库排序 Recall@20 相对 SOTA 提升约 3–4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/全库排序&quot;,&quot;ODE 图滤波&quot;,&quot;Blurring-Sharpening&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/collaborative_filtering/blurring_sharpening_process_models_for_collaborative_filtering/">Blurring-Sharpening Process Models for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免训练 ODE 模糊-锐化范式直接处理交互矩阵，在三个基准全库排序 Recall/NDCG 超 43 个基线，NDCG 最高提升 3.74%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回（全库排序）&quot;,&quot;变分图重构&quot;,&quot;聚类感知对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/self_supervised_denoising/generative_contrastive_graph_learning_for_recommendation/">Generative-Contrastive Graph Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以变分图重构采样替代数据增广生成对比视图，联合节点/簇级对比，在三个数据集 Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@20 较 LightGCN 提升 8.61%–28.17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图结构重连 (Graph Rewiring)&quot;,&quot;双边 Top-K 去噪与增广&quot;,&quot;用户-用户/物品-物品相关性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/graph_rec/graph_collaborative_signals_denoising_and_augmentation_for_recommendation/">Graph Collaborative Signals Denoising and Augmentation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>预训练后双边 Top-K 重连二部图并补入用户-用户/物品-物品对称相关块，Amazon 全库排序 NDCG@20 相对提升 6.9%–13.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Graph Transformer&quot;,&quot;Masked Autoencoding&quot;,&quot;Rationale Discovery&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/self_supervised_denoising/graph_transformer_for_recommendation/">Graph Transformer for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GFormer 用拓扑感知图 Transformer 参数化发现协同 rationale 子图，并以 rationale-aware 掩码自编码替代随机对比增广；全库排序下 Recall@20 较 SGL 提升 5.2%–7.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何表征&quot;,&quot;线性化图 Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/hgformer_hyperbolic_graph_transformer_for_recommendation/">Hgformer: Hyperbolic Graph Transformer for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双曲流形上的无参图卷积 + 线性化双曲交叉注意力，兼顾长尾局部结构与全局信息，全库排序下 Amazon-Book R@10 相对提升 17.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回与排序表征学习&quot;,&quot;知识图谱剪枝&quot;,&quot;对比去噪&quot;,&quot;伯努利掩码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/self_supervised_denoising/knowledge_refined_denoising_network_for_robust_recommendation/">Knowledge-refined Denoising Network for Robust Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KRDN 用伯努利掩码剪枝 KG 任务无关三元组，并以协同/知识信号分歧对比式剔除噪声交互；全库排序下 NDCG@20 较 SOTA 提升 4.6%–12.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;逻辑查询嵌入 (LQE)&quot;,&quot;多任务知识共享&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/graph_rec/logicrec_recommendation_with_users_logical_requirements/">LogicRec: Recommendation with Users&#x27; Logical Requirements</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户逻辑需求形式化为 KG 结构化逻辑查询，用 LQE 联合推断缺失事实并以多任务多门专家共享 A_l/A_u 答案集，Amazon-book hit@20 相对提升约 22%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为建模&quot;,&quot;梯度冲突矫正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/self_supervised_denoising/multi_behavior_self_supervised_learning_for_recommendation/">Multi-behavior Self-supervised Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>行为感知GNN+双层自判别对比+梯度混合矫正，Leave-One-Out全库排序下Recall@10平均提升约19%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;策略学习与交互决策&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;跨视图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/agent_conversational_rec/multi_view_hypergraph_contrastive_policy_learning_for_conversational_recomm/">Multi-view Hypergraph Contrastive Policy Learning for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用三视图动态超图+分层超图编码+跨视图对比策略学习建模对话推荐中的多源用户偏好，Yelp 上 SR@15 提升 18.28%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端训练/召回&quot;,&quot;Sharpness-Aware Minimization&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;,&quot;隐函数微分&quot;,&quot;泛化与长尾&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/graph_rec/sharpness_aware_graph_collaborative_filtering/">Sharpness-Aware Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双层优化+隐函数超梯度把锐度感知训练引入GNN协同过滤，全库排序下LightGCN骨干Recall@20最高提升13.48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;增量SVD&quot;,&quot;图信号处理低通滤波&quot;,&quot;可解释交互推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/graph_rec/triple_structural_information_modelling_for_accurate_explainable_and_intera/">Triple Structural Information Modelling for Accurate, Explainable and Interactive Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>增量SVD低通滤波＋无参数注意力＋物品转移矩阵三源融合，全库排序 HR@10 最高相对提升 203.8%，并支持频率级可解释交互推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;特征过相关&quot;,&quot;过平滑&quot;,&quot;自适应正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/afdgcf_adaptive_feature_de_correlation_graph_collaborative_filtering_for_re/">AFDGCF: Adaptive Feature De-correlation Graph Collaborative Filtering for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 GNN-CF 中被忽视的“特征过相关”问题并证明其与过平滑成正比，提出层级自适应的列相关惩罚正则，模型无关地提升 4 类骨干模型最多 14.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;行为模式挖掘&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯似然比&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/collaborative_filtering/behavior_pattern_mining_based_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Behavior Pattern Mining-based Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以行为模式路径计数为特征、贝叶斯似然比闭式打分替代GNN，在三个电商数据集Full-Ranking下Recall@10最高提升393.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图卷积&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;位置编码解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/cadrec_contextualized_and_debiased_recommender_model/">CaDRec: Contextualized and Debiased Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以超图卷积融合序列自注意力抑制过平滑，并用位置编码流行度与个体偏差双路解耦及梯度加权重整实现去偏，全库排序下 R@20 相对提升 4.2%–4.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;持续图学习&quot;,&quot;图提示微调&quot;,&quot;流式推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/gpt4rec_graph_prompt_tuning_for_streaming_recommendation/">GPT4Rec: Graph Prompt Tuning for Streaming Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将演化交互图解耦为多视图，用节点/结构/视图三级轻量图提示替代回放与模型扩张，实现流式推荐持续学习，R@20 最高相对提升 4.16%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图信号处理&quot;,&quot;扩散模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/generative_rec/graph_signal_diffusion_model_for_collaborative_filtering/">Graph Signal Diffusion Model for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用物品相似图上的热方程平滑替代高斯加噪作为前向扩散，反向用确定性去噪器做精修-锐化重建，三数据集全库排序 SOTA，Recall@20 最高 +5.44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;参数高效嵌入&quot;,&quot;元嵌入组合&quot;,&quot;矩阵伪逆闭式更新&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/lightweight_embeddings_for_graph_collaborative_filtering/">Lightweight Embeddings for Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以可学习稀疏分配矩阵替代固定哈希映射，在扩展图上联合优化元嵌入码本，实现 44× 参数压缩下接近全量嵌入的精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;线性化核注意力&quot;,&quot;结构位置编码&quot;,&quot;度数重加权掩码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/masked_graph_transformer_for_large_scale_recommendation/">Masked Graph Transformer for Large-Scale Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>纯 Transformer 全节点注意力 + SimRFs 线性核 + 可学习相对度数掩码，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 8.62%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;负反馈感知超图卷积&quot;,&quot;Transformer Hawkes Process&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/nfarec_a_negative_feedback_aware_recommender_model/">NFARec: A Negative Feedback-Aware Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以反馈极性引导超图卷积消息通路并用 Transformer Hawkes 过程辅助预测下一交互情感极性，五数据集全库排序 R@20 最高提升 6.6%、N@20 提升 14.2%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图基准数据集&quot;,&quot;选址推荐&quot;,&quot;全库排序评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/opensiterec_an_open_dataset_for_site_recommendation/">OpenSiteRec: An Open Dataset for Site Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个公开选址推荐数据集，用 5 类实体 9 类关系的异构图刻画 4 座国际都市，并在 5-core + 全库排序协议下基准测试 16 个推荐模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;个性化自增强学习&quot;,&quot;短期协作图&quot;,&quot;序列去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/sequential_rec/selfgnn_self_supervised_graph_neural_networks_for_sequential_recommendation/">SelfGNN: Self-Supervised Graph Neural Networks for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>按时间切片构建短期协作图并用个性化自增强学习以长期兴趣矫正短期噪声，1:999 采样排序下四个数据集全面超越 17 个基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表示学习&quot;,&quot;符号图&quot;,&quot;图Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/sigformer_sign_aware_graph_transformer_for_recommendation/">SIGformer: Sign-aware Graph Transformer for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用Transformer直接建模整张符号图，以符号谱/路径编码融合正负反馈，五数据集Recall@20最高提升15.61%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;注意力采样&quot;,&quot;结构位置编码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/transgnn_harnessing_the_collaborative_power_of_transformers_and_graph_neura/">TransGNN: Harnessing the Collaborative Power of Transformers and Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>交替堆叠 Transformer 与 GNN 层，用注意力采样+三种结构位置编码互相扩感受野与注入拓扑，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多项式图滤波&quot;,&quot;免训练协同过滤&quot;,&quot;低通滤波&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/graph_rec/turbo_cf_matrix_decomposition_free_graph_filtering_for_fast_recommendation/">Turbo-CF: Matrix Decomposition-Free Graph Filtering for Fast Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多项式图滤波器替代特征分解实现免训练免分解的协同过滤，全库排序下精度持平SOTA而耗时降至亚秒级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图提示 (Graph Prompting)&quot;,&quot;负迁移抑制&quot;,&quot;多域预训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/adaptive_graph_integration_for_cross_domain_recommendation_via_heterogeneou/">Adaptive Graph Integration for Cross-Domain Recommendation via Heterogeneous Graph Coordinators</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用异质自适应图协调器把多域图拼成统一结构做多域预训练，再以图提示迁移到目标域，Full-Ranking 下全面超越 9 个基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱构建&quot;,&quot;跨类型资源推荐&quot;,&quot;模型血缘追踪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/graph_rec/benchmarking_recommendation_classification_and_tracing_based_on_hugging_fac/">Benchmarking Recommendation, Classification, and Tracing Based on Hugging Face Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 260 万节点 HuggingKG 与三任务 HuggingBench，揭示稀疏领域 KG 上 KGRec 类方法失效、TransE 反超复杂模型的关键现象。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双阶段模态融合&quot;,&quot;复合图卷积&quot;,&quot;自适应BPR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/multimodal_rec/cohesion_composite_graph_convolutional_network_with_dual_stage_fusion_for_m/">COHESION: Composite Graph Convolutional Network with Dual-Stage Fusion for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用行为模态早期精炼+晚期注意力融合的双阶段融合，配合异质/同质复合图与自适应BPR，在三个 Amazon 数据集上 R@20 相对提升 4.4%–6.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱增强推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;检索增强表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/graph_rec/comprehending_knowledge_graphs_with_large_language_models_for_recommender_s/">Comprehending Knowledge Graphs with Large Language Models for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 理解 KG 局部子图并构建语义物品图做全局检索增强，离线预计算语义嵌入后接入 LightGCN，全库排序下全面超越 12 个基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;由粗到细检索&quot;,&quot;子图检索&quot;,&quot;LLM推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/corona_a_coarse_to_fine_framework_for_graph_based_recommendation_with_large/">CORONA: A Coarse-to-Fine Framework for Graph-based Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 做偏好/意图推理在交互图上逐级缩小候选子图，再由 GNN 精排，全库排序下 Recall 平均相对提升 18.6%。</p>
</dd>
