<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱滤波&quot;,&quot;切比雪夫插值&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/collaborative_filtering/graph_spectral_filtering_with_chebyshev_interpolation_for_recommendation/">Graph Spectral Filtering with Chebyshev Interpolation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用切比雪夫插值逼近非线性图滤波器，直接以原始交互为信号，突破 LightGCN 的截断线性低通瓶颈，Full-Ranking 下全面 SOTA 且推理快 8.1 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间推荐&quot;,&quot;元优化两阶段训练&quot;,&quot;层级信息对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/large_language_models_enhanced_hyperbolic_space_recommender_systems/">Large Language Models Enhanced Hyperbolic Space Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HyperLLM 用 LLM 生成层级标签与偏好摘要，经元优化两阶段训练将结构/语义层级信息注入双曲推荐，Recall@20 最高提升 41.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;机器遗忘&quot;,&quot;图推荐&quot;,&quot;影响编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/pre_training_for_recommendation_unlearning/">Pre-training for Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出预训练式影响编码器 UnlearnRec，将遗忘请求映射为嵌入漂移，免重训实现高效图推荐遗忘，10x 加速且保持精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR-ICTIR 2025&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;相似边对 (SEP)&quot;,&quot;边感知图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/sep_gcn_leveraging_similar_edge_pairs_with_temporal_and_spatial_contexts_fo/">SEP-GCN: Leveraging Similar Edge Pairs with Temporal and Spatial Contexts for Location-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以时空相似交互边对构建边级相似图并注入LightGCN传播，在NYC/Gowalla/Brightkite全库排序上Recall@20最高提升14.89%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;SENet特征重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/self_supervised_denoising/squeeze_and_excitation_a_weighted_graph_contrastive_learning_for_collaborat/">Squeeze and Excitation: A Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>仅扰动末层GCN视图并用SENet动态加权特征，在三个数据集上实现最高24.45%的NDCG@20提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Customer Persona&quot;,&quot;Random Walk Approximation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/you_are_what_you_bought_generating_customer_personas_for_e_commerce_applica/">You Are What You Bought: Generating Customer Personas for E-commerce Applications</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 标注少量原型用户、随机游走传播生成可读人物画像，并以 RevAff 近似加速，画像注入 GCN 推荐最高提升 12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Aspect-Conditioned Scoring&quot;,&quot;异构图消息传递&quot;,&quot;作者谱系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/graph_rec/aspect_aware_content_based_recommendations_for_mathematical_research_papers/">Aspect-Aware Content-Based Recommendations for Mathematical Research Papers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向数学论文的 aspect 条件异构图推荐，构建 GoldRiM/SilverRiM 数据集，AchGNN 在 Full-Ranking 下 MRR 相对 GraphCL 提升 26%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双通道图学习&quot;,&quot;频率感知门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/graph_rec/dcgl_dual_channel_graph_learning_with_large_language_models_for_knowledge_a/">DCGL: Dual-Channel Graph Learning with Large Language Models for Knowledge-Aware Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双通道解耦 LLM 语义与 ID 行为信号，多级对比对齐 + 频率感知门控融合，四数据集全库排序全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;可复现性基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/from_top_1_to_top_k_a_reproducibility_study_and_benchmarking_of_counterfact/">From Top-1 to Top-K: A Reproducibility Study and Benchmarking of Counterfactual Explanations for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一复现11种反事实解释器，首次提出列表级Top-K评估协议与PN-R位置敏感指标，揭示显式格式下有效性-稀疏性权衡与图解释器可扩展性瓶颈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;问答推荐&quot;,&quot;多项式图滤波&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/graph_rec/qagcf_graph_collaborative_filtering_for_q_a_recommendation/">QAGCF: Graph Collaborative Filtering for Q&amp;A Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将问答对解耦为用户-问题/用户-答案双协同图与问题-答案/问题-问题双语义图，融合为全局异质图后用 Jacobi 多项式图滤波器分离高低频信号，配合噪声对比学习，在知乎与商业数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@10 提升 5.16%–5.34%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;模态补全&quot;,&quot;子图检索&quot;,&quot;图 Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multimodal_rec/robust_multimodal_recommendation_via_graph_retrieval_enhanced_modality_comp/">Robust Multimodal Recommendation via Graph Retrieval-Enhanced Modality Completion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模态感知子图检索替代 K 跳邻居聚合，配合图 Transformer 与稀疏路由码本补全缺失模态，40% 缺失率下 Recall@20 相对提升 4.2%–5.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时间即算子&quot;,&quot;谱滤波&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multimodal_rec/timemm_time_as_operator_spectral_filtering_for_dynamic_multimodal_recommend/">TimeMM: Time-as-Operator Spectral Filtering for Dynamic Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将交互新近度映射为时间核加权图算子族，隐式实现免特征分解的多尺度谱滤波，配合自适应算子混合与模态路由，全库排序下 R@20 相对提升 3%–10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;小波包变换&quot;,&quot;子带图传播&quot;,&quot;时频多分辨率建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/sequential_rec/wpgrec_wavelet_packet_guided_graph_enhanced_sequential_recommendation/">WPGRec: Wavelet Packet Guided Graph Enhanced Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用平稳小波包变换生成等长平移不变子带，在子带内做 Chebyshev 图传播并以能量-谱平坦度门控融合，Full-Ranking 下稀疏数据集 HR@20 最高 +15.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/评分预测&quot;,&quot;元图&quot;,&quot;因子分解机&quot;,&quot;异构信息网络&quot;,&quot;辅助信息融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2021/graph_rec/side_information_fusion_for_recommender_systems_over_heterogeneous_informat/">Side Information Fusion for Recommender Systems over Heterogeneous Information Network</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出元图相似度与“MF+FM”/HAF 融合异构辅助信息，在 4 个评分预测数据集上 RMSE 显著优于元路径基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨域序列推荐&quot;,&quot;知识图谱迁移&quot;,&quot;门控信息流&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2022/cross_domain_multi_scenario/mixed_information_flow_for_cross_domain_sequential_recommendations/">Mixed Information Flow for Cross-domain Sequential Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出行为流+知识流双通道 MIFN，配跨域图卷积与模式开关，四域全库排序 MRR@10 提升约 2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;解耦表征学习&quot;,&quot;通道感知图聚合&quot;,&quot;变分自编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2023/sequential_rec/edge_enhanced_global_disentangled_graph_neural_network_for_sequential_recom/">Edge-Enhanced Global Disentangled Graph Neural Network for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用通道感知解耦把物品转移边拆成 K 个潜在因子通道，全局链路图 + 局部 SA-VAE 滑窗双层级学习用户意图，ML-1M N@5 相对提升 8.21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;耦合张量分解&quot;,&quot;图正则SNMF&quot;,&quot;ADMM&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2021/collaborative_filtering/coupled_graphs_and_tensor_factorization_for_recommender_systems_and_communi/">Coupled Graphs and Tensor Factorization for Recommender Systems and Community Detection</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CGTF：PARAFAC 张量与对角缩放 SNMF 图耦合，ADMM 闭式补全缺失评分与图链接，40% 缺失下 NMSE 降 53.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图&quot;,&quot;元路径&quot;,&quot;注意力融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2021/graph_rec/deep_collaborative_filtering_with_multi_aspect_information_in_heterogeneous/">Deep Collaborative Filtering with Multi-Aspect Information in Heterogeneous Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用元路径抽取方面级相似度，经 MLP 学习多视角隐因子并以注意力/自注意力融合，在 Leave-One-Out+99 负采样下 HR/NDCG 显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱嵌入&quot;,&quot;元路径与嵌入传播&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/graph_rec/a_survey_on_knowledge_graph_based_recommender_systems/">A Survey on Knowledge Graph-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以“嵌入/路径/统一”三范式归档 39 篇 KG 推荐工作，梳理 7 类场景数据集、可解释性来源与开放问题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;社交影响扩散&quot;,&quot;多层图注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/social_group_rec/diffnet_a_neural_influence_and_interest_diffusion_network_for_social_recomm/">DiffNet++: A Neural Influence and Interest Diffusion Network for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将社交图与兴趣图统一为异构图，用多层注意力递归扩散影响与兴趣，采样排序下 HR@10 较最强基线提升 21%+</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;模体GCN&quot;,&quot;对抗训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/social_group_rec/enhancing_social_recommendation_with_adversarial_graph_convolutional_networ/">Enhancing Social Recommendation with Adversarial Graph Convolutional Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>模体GCN生成替代邻居、重构去噪与情境注意力，对抗训练统一；Full-Ranking下NDCG@10提升6.75%–7.73%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;高阶社交传播&quot;,&quot;层级注意力聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/social_group_rec/modelling_high_order_social_relations_for_item_recommendation/">Modelling High-Order Social Relations for Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多层 GCN 沿社交图递归传播用户嵌入以注入高阶好友影响，再用注意力聚合各层输出；在 Yelp/Douban 全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 22.28%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;自我网络&quot;,&quot;分层图池化&quot;,&quot;谱域图滤波&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/social_group_rec/understanding_wechat_user_preferences_and_wow_diffusion/">Understanding WeChat User Preferences and \&quot;Wow\&quot; Diffusion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于微信看一看海量数据揭示好友圈层结构对\&quot;Wow\&quot;与点击行为的相反影响，提出谱域滤波+分层图池化的DiffuseGNN预测模型，AUC最高提升约2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/粗排&quot;,&quot;神经推荐综述&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;,&quot;辅助信息融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/collaborative_filtering/a_survey_on_accuracy_oriented_neural_recommendation_from_collaborative_filt/">A Survey on Accuracy-oriented Neural Recommendation: From Collaborative Filtering to Information-rich Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以数据使用为轴重构神经推荐综述：统一框架下划分协同过滤、内容增强与时空序列三类，梳理表征/交互建模与辅助信息融合演进及开放问题</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异构图嵌入&quot;,&quot;元素级注意力&quot;,&quot;个性化训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/a_unified_framework_for_cross_domain_and_cross_system_recommendations/">A Unified Framework for Cross-Domain and Cross-System Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出GA统一框架，以异构图嵌入、元素级注意力与个性化训练同时提升DTCDR/MTCDR/CDR+CSR，1:99采样排序下平均超越SOTA 9.04%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;反事实去偏&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/graph_rec/causal_inference_for_knowledge_graph_based_recommendation/">Causal Inference for Knowledge Graph based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果图拆解 KG 推荐中结构混淆与属性捷径偏差，以 do 干预+反事实推断实现去偏属性偏好建模，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 提升 3.79%~6.66%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱注意力&quot;,&quot;偏置随机游走&quot;,&quot;门控上下文融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/graph_rec/contextualized_graph_attention_network_for_recommendation_with_item_knowled/">Contextualized Graph Attention Network for Recommendation with Item Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以用户特定图注意力聚合 KG 局部邻居、偏置随机游走+GRU 抽取非局部高阶上下文、物品特定注意力建模历史交互；FM/ML/BC 6:2:2 划分全库排序下 HR@20 较最强基线提升 8.3%/1.0%/5.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;动态图神经网络&quot;,&quot;跨序列协同信号&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/sequential_rec/dynamic_graph_neural_networks_for_sequential_recommendation/">Dynamic Graph Neural Networks for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将全部用户序列编织成带时间戳与双向顺序的五元组动态图，用相对顺序感知注意力+短期注意力做子图消息传播，把 next-item 预测转为动态图链路预测，Beauty 上 NDCG@10 相对提升 10.56%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE 2023&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排（评分预测 + Top-N 排序）&quot;,&quot;去中心化图&quot;,&quot;偏置向量化&quot;,&quot;社交关系加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/social_group_rec/gdsrec_graph_based_decentralized_collaborative_filtering_for_social_recomme/">GDSRec: Graph-Based Decentralized Collaborative Filtering for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把评分偏置建成向量并构造去中心化图，配合偏好相似度加权的可微社交强度；Ciao/Epinions 上 MAE 相对 DiffNet++ 降低 0.9%–3.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;哈希学习 (Learning to Hash)&quot;,&quot;汉明空间图卷积&quot;,&quot;高阶相似度传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/graph_rec/hs_gcn_hamming_spatial_graph_convolutional_networks_for_recommendation/">HS-GCN: Hamming Spatial Graph Convolutional Networks for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在汉明空间直接做图卷积传播，显式建模一阶与高阶汉明相似度，7 个数据集全库排序 HR@50 平均超最强哈希基线 26.2%、逼近实值 GNN 且训练/推理提速 2.4×/4.9×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲表示学习&quot;,&quot;语义邻居聚合&quot;,&quot;陀螺向量空间&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/collaborative_filtering/hyperbolic_neural_collaborative_recommender/">Hyperbolic Neural Collaborative Recommender</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用热核加权关系图构造用户/物品语义邻居，并在 Poincaré 球内以陀螺向量算子做深度聚合，CTR AUC 较最强基线最高 +4.15%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;评论图构建&quot;,&quot;个性化图池化&quot;,&quot;类型感知图注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/graph_rec/learning_hierarchical_review_graph_representations_for_recommendation/">Learning Hierarchical Review Graph Representations for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>为每个用户/物品单独构建带词序边类型的评论图，用类型感知图注意力与个性化图池化学习层级语义表征，6 个 Amazon/Yelp 数据集上 MSE 较最强基线 DAML 平均再降 1.14%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;元学习 MAML&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/cold_start_rec/metakg_meta_learning_on_knowledge_graph_for_cold_start_recommendation/">MetaKG: Meta-learning on Knowledge Graph for Cold-start Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双元学习器（协同感知局部更新 + 知识感知全局更新）配自适应任务调度，在 UC/IC/UIC 三类冷启动场景全库排序下平均提升 62%–75%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;多行为序列推荐&quot;,&quot;时间图Transformer&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/sequential_rec/multi_behavior_sequential_recommendation_with_temporal_graph_transformer/">Multi-Behavior Sequential Recommendation with Temporal Graph Transformer</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>行为感知Transformer与时间图联合建模多行为短长期依赖，HR@10较MBGCN提升22.5%/12.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;排序&quot;,&quot;强化学习路径探索&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/sequential_rec/reinforcement_learning_based_path_exploration_for_sequential_explainable_re/">Reinforcement Learning based Path Exploration for Sequential Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TMER-RL 用强化学习探索连续物品间元路径，并以双注意力融合时序与路径解释，在 4 个数据集 500 负采样排序上显著优于 TMER。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;会话推荐&quot;,&quot;解耦图神经网络&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/sequential_rec/transition_information_enhanced_disentangled_graph_neural_networks_for_sess/">Transition Information Enhanced Disentangled Graph Neural Networks for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>位置感知全局图+解耦因子GNN，双通道对比学习建模会话转移，Tmall P@20提升11.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图结构学习&quot;,&quot;层次原型&quot;,&quot;对比多视图融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/bi_level_graph_structure_learning_for_next_poi_recommendation/">Bi-Level Graph Structure Learning for Next POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 EM 聚类原型构造 POI–原型层次图，并配自适应成对结构学习与多视图对比融合；全库排序下 Acc@20 最高相对提升 13.56%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;,&quot;属性推断攻击&quot;,&quot;自适应隐私预算&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/comprehensive_privacy_analysis_on_federated_recommender_system_against_attr/">Comprehensive Privacy Analysis on Federated Recommender System against Attribute Inference Attacks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>先用梯度式属性推断攻击逐组件量化 FedRec 泄漏差异，再提出按脆弱度分配隐私预算的自适应 LDP 机制 APM，在几乎不损 Hit@20 的前提下显著降低属性推断 F1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;神经常微分方程&quot;,&quot;动态图推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/sequential_rec/learning_graph_ode_for_continuous_time_sequential_recommendation/">Learning Graph ODE for Continuous-Time Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GDERec 用自回归图 ODE 建模用户-物品交互图的连续演化，配时间注意力聚合不规则采样，5 数据集全库排序 R@10 提升约 8.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;马尔可夫图扩散&quot;,&quot;InfoBPR 排序损失&quot;,&quot;图稀疏化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/graph_rec/mgdcf_distance_learning_via_markov_graph_diffusion_for_neural_collaborative/">MGDCF: Distance Learning via Markov Graph Diffusion for Neural Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明 LightGCN/APPNP 等价于 1 层上下文编码 NRL，用马尔可夫图扩散权衡两类距离，并配 InfoBPR 多负样本损失，Amazon-Book NDCG@20 较 LightGCN 提升 47%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;监督对比损失&quot;,&quot;协同邻居&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/self_supervised_denoising/neighborhood_enhanced_supervised_contrastive_learning_for_collaborative_fil/">Neighborhood-Enhanced Supervised Contrastive Learning for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 ItemKNN 最近邻与交互邻居纳入对比损失正样本，缓解假阴性；三数据集全库排序 NDCG@20 较 SGL 提升 10.09%/7.09%/35.36%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排与可解释生成&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;知识图谱路径推理&quot;,&quot;强化学习策略梯度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/graph_rec/reinforced_path_reasoning_for_counterfactual_explainable_recommendation/">Reinforced Path Reasoning for Counterfactual Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TKDE 2024｜图神经网络与知识图谱推荐｜在协同知识图谱上用两步注意力路径采样器压缩动作空间，以强化学习优化反事实解释策略，生成物品属性级反事实解释并反哺推荐训练（Yelp2018 Recall@80 +78.03%）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自监督预训练&quot;,&quot;数据增广&quot;,&quot;InfoNCE&quot;,&quot;SELFRec&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/self_supervised_denoising/self_supervised_learning_for_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Self-Supervised Learning for Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次系统界定自监督推荐 (SSR)，提出对比/生成/预测/混合四分类与 JL/PF/IL 三训练范式，并开源 SELFRec 在 Full-Ranking 下给出受控实证结论。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回/全库排序&quot;,&quot;图对比学习&quot;,&quot;噪声嵌入增广&quot;,&quot;跨层对比&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/self_supervised_denoising/xsimgcl_towards_extremely_simple_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendat/">XSimGCL: Towards Extremely Simple Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用噪声嵌入增广与单编码器跨层对比替代图增广，四数据集全库排序显著提升且训练近 LightGCN 轻量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;关系链对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/graph_rec/dual_channel_multiplex_graph_neural_networks_for_recommendation/">Dual-Channel Multiplex Graph Neural Networks for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双通道多路图网络显式建模行为模式、隐式挖掘关系链依赖，三数据集全指标平均提升 10%+</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;知识图谱路径推理&quot;,&quot;分层强化学习&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/sequential_rec/explainable_session_based_recommendation_via_path_reasoning/">Explainable Session-based Recommendation via Path Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出分层强化学习框架 PR4SR，用会话级智能体选起点、路径级智能体做知识图谱推理，配合多目标与路径中点奖励，为 5 种 SR 模型提供可解释路径并提升精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;交叉相关聚合&quot;,&quot;非递归图表示&quot;,&quot;多跳语义解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/graph_rec/graph_cross_correlated_network_for_recommendation/">Graph Cross-Correlated Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用非递归 PGR 保留各跳邻居纯语义，再以 HCC/ECC 显式建模用户-物品子图交叉相关项，全库排序下 NDCG@20 平均提升约 1.8%–2.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;元路径意图建模&quot;,&quot;信息瓶颈去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/graph_rec/intent_guided_heterogeneous_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">Intent-guided Heterogeneous Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用元路径意图构建双对比学习视图，并以信息瓶颈掩码自编码器去噪，六数据集全库排序 Recall@20 最高提升 12.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图压缩&quot;,&quot;二分结构损失&quot;,&quot;梯度匹配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/graph_rec/democratic_recommendation_with_user_and_item_representatives_produced_by_gr/">Democratic Recommendation with User and Item Representatives Produced by Graph Condensation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用图压缩生成用户/物品代表，并以二分结构损失 BSL 约束压缩图，80% 数据量下 P@20 仅降 8.5% 且训练快于 LightGCN。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Krylov 子空间&quot;,&quot;多项式图滤波&quot;,&quot;免训练推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/collaborative_filtering/memory_is_no_longer_a_bottleneck_memory_efficient_graph_filtering_for_scala/">Memory Is No Longer a Bottleneck: Memory-Efficient Graph Filtering for Scalable Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用逐用户 Krylov 子空间替代全量物品相似度图，实现免训练多项式图滤波，内存降 5.74×、推理加速 26× 且精度反超 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;小波分解&quot;,&quot;模态融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/multimodal_rec/tackling_the_modality_inhibitance_effect_with_wavelet_based_modality_integr/">Tackling the Modality Inhibitance Effect with Wavelet-based Modality Integration in Multi-modal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出模态抑制效应(MIE)概念，用Haar小波多分辨率分解提取各模态本质特征、结构感知多模态图学习与交叉加权自适应调节器，在4数据集Full-Ranking下平均提升6.48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图特征插值&quot;,&quot;缺失模态填补&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/multimodal_rec/training_free_graph_based_imputation_of_missing_modalities_in_multimodal_re/">Training-free Graph-based Imputation of Missing Modalities in Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将缺失模态重铸为物品-物品共购图上的图特征插值，提出四种免训练传播式填补算子，可即插即用并保持甚至扩大多模态与传统推荐的性能差距。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TMM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多模态融合&quot;,&quot;图卷积推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TMM/2026/multimodal_rec/a_survey_on_multimodal_recommender_systems_recent_advances_and_future_direc/">A Survey on Multimodal Recommender Systems: Recent Advances and Future Directions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Multimedia">IEEE TMM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以「特征提取→编码器→多模态融合→损失函数」四段式技术流水线重构 48 篇 MRS 工作分类，提出融合时机×策略正交视角并开源配套仓库。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;位置编码&quot;,&quot;会话图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2022/sequential_rec/exploiting_positional_information_for_session_based_recommendation/">Exploiting Positional Information for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双位置编码DPE/LDPE与位置感知门控图网络PosRec，全库排序下在Yoochoose/Diginetica稳定超越SOTA约0.4%–2.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图超边嵌入&quot;,&quot;偶发群组推荐&quot;,&quot;社交同质性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2022/social_group_rec/hierarchical_hyperedge_embedding_based_representation_learning_for_group_re/">Hierarchical Hyperedge Embedding-based Representation Learning for Group Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HyperGroup 用社交图 GNN 增强个体偏好、把群组建模为超图超边做归纳式嵌入，全库排序下 Yelp HR@5 较最强基线提升 268%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图预训练&quot;,&quot;局部子图元学习&quot;,&quot;FiLM 图级适配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/graph_rec/an_adaptive_graph_pre_training_framework_for_localized_collaborative_filter/">An Adaptive Graph Pre-training Framework for Localized Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用局部子图替代用户/物品嵌入做可迁移的元 GNN，并由图属性驱动 adaptor 生成定制 GCN，稀疏目标图上 HR@5 最高提升 81.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异质信息网络&quot;,&quot;课程式预训练&quot;,&quot;子图对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/graph_rec/curriculum_pre_training_heterogeneous_subgraph_transformer_for_top_n_recomm/">Curriculum Pre-Training Heterogeneous Subgraph Transformer for Top-N Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>交互特定异质子图 + 复合槽位嵌入 Transformer + 由易到难课程式预训练，Leave-One-Out/1000 负采样下 HR@20 较最强基线提升 9.9%–25.0%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图表征解耦&quot;,&quot;多目标跨域迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/heterogeneous_graph_based_framework_with_disentangled_representations_learn/">Heterogeneous Graph-based Framework with Disentangled Representations Learning for Multi-target Cross Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以单一异构全局图建模多域用户/物品，用域特异与域共享双通道图卷积解耦表征，多域同时提升 HR@10 最高约 10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多模态语义实体图&quot;,&quot;情感加权图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/multimodal_rec/megcf_multimodal_entity_graph_collaborative_filtering_for_personalized_reco/">MEGCF: Multimodal Entity Graph Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用语义实体替代完整深度特征并构建情感加权对称线性GCN，缓解MFE与UIM失配；留一法+99负采样下NDCG@10最高提升8.87%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;变分自编码器&quot;,&quot;乘积专家(PoE)&quot;,&quot;归纳式图自编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/cold_start_rec/multi_auxiliary_augmented_collaborative_variational_auto_encoder_for_tag_re/">Multi-Auxiliary Augmented Collaborative Variational Auto-encoder for Tag Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多项式VAE、内容VAE与归纳式VGAE经PoE融合及MSE互约束耦合协同与多源辅助信息，在ml-20m/citeulike全标签库排序下Recall@20提升55%–99%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时间感知图卷积&quot;,&quot;高阶连通性&quot;,&quot;自注意力聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/sequential_rec/position_enhanced_and_time_aware_graph_convolutional_network_for_sequential/">Position-enhanced and Time-aware Graph Convolutional Network for Sequential Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在用户-物品二部图上定义位置增强/时间感知图卷积与自注意力聚合器，同步建模用户与物品的时序动态并堆叠多层捕获高阶连通性，Movielens/Amazon R@10 较 DGCF 提升约 9-11%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;MIPS 最大内积检索&quot;,&quot;近邻图路由&quot;,&quot;强化学习与奖励塑形&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/collaborative_filtering/reinforcement_routing_on_proximity_graph_for_efficient_recommendation/">Reinforcement Routing on Proximity Graph for Efficient Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Reinforce+自评基线训练 GCN 智能体在近邻图上路由，并以势函数奖励塑形注入少量 ground truth；固定 IPC 预算下全库召回全面超越 ip-NSW/IPDG/Mobius。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/可解释排序&quot;,&quot;时间感知知识图谱&quot;,&quot;强化学习路径推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/graph_rec/time_aware_path_reasoning_on_knowledge_graph_for_recommendation/">Time-aware Path Reasoning on Knowledge Graph for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>时间戳聚类构建TCKG，个性化时间奖励引导RL路径推理，Amazon@10最高较ADAC提升14.71% NDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;异质超图&quot;,&quot;双偏好学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/sequential_rec/bi_preference_learning_heterogeneous_hypergraph_networks_for_session_based_/">Bi-Preference Learning Heterogeneous Hypergraph Networks for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用类别内逻辑斯谛价格分档与异质超图三层卷积，联合建模价格/兴趣双偏好，在三个会话数据集全库排序下稳定超越强基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;级联残差GCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/graph_rec/cascading_residual_graph_convolutional_network_for_multi_behavior_recommend/">Cascading Residual Graph Convolutional Network for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>无额外参数的级联残差GCN按行为顺序精化嵌入，三数据集全库排序HR@K平均较最优基线提升24.76%/27.28%/25.10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;软节点聚类&quot;,&quot;Gumbel-Softmax&quot;,&quot;过平滑&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/graph_rec/cluster_based_graph_collaborative_filtering/">Cluster-based Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Gumbel-Softmax 软节点聚类构建簇特定图，将高阶图卷积限制在同兴趣簇内以滤除噪声并缓解过平滑，Recall@20 相对 SOTA 提升 2.2%–4.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2024&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序&quot;,&quot;图提示微调&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;参数高效迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/contrastive_graph_prompt_tuning_for_cross_domain_recommendation/">Contrastive Graph Prompt-tuning for Cross-domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结GNN、仅调个性化图提示的跨域Top-K推荐；四组Amazon域对最高降74%调参，冷启动+11.41%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;表征学习/召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;数据增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/self_supervised_denoising/contrastive_self_supervised_learning_in_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Contrastive Self-supervised Learning in Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>综述提出统一框架与三维分类，系统梳理视图生成、对比任务与目标，并给出组件选择指南与开放问题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;事后解释与公平性诊断&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;离线强化学习&quot;,&quot;异构图属性归因&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/counterfactual_explanation_for_fairness_in_recommendation/">Counterfactual Explanation for Fairness in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用异构图属性节点做反事实解释，以离线强化学习搜索最小属性集解释曝光不公平，擦除评估下 NDCG 与 Gini 双优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;自监督对比与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多视图对比学习&quot;,&quot;捆绑包推荐&quot;,&quot;图卷积网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/self_supervised_denoising/multicbr_multi_view_contrastive_learning_for_bundle_recommendation/">MultiCBR: Multi-view Contrastive Learning for Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出三视图(UB/UI/BI)表征与“先融合后对比”框架，BI 双向图传播破解捆绑包稀疏，iFashion 上 Recall@20 相对提升 32.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;采样Softmax损失&quot;,&quot;难负样本挖掘&quot;,&quot;图卷积网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/collaborative_filtering/on_the_effectiveness_of_sampled_softmax_loss_for_item_recommendation/">On the Effectiveness of Sampled Softmax Loss for Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论揭示SSM损失缓解流行度偏差、挖掘难负样本并对齐DCG，图消息传递可补偿其模长学习缺陷；LightGCN+SSM全库排序Recall@20最高提升41.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;知识利用率&quot;,&quot;鲁棒性评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/graph_rec/kg4receval_does_knowledge_graph_really_matter_for_recommender_systems/">KG4RecEval: Does Knowledge Graph Really Matter for Recommender Systems?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KGER 指标与 KG4RecEval 框架，系统扰动/删除 KG 知识，发现 KG 对推荐精度贡献常为负或近零，挑战 KG 必有益的主流认知。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;组合嵌入量化&quot;,&quot;图重连稀疏化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/collaborative_filtering/lightweight_embeddings_with_graph_rewiring_for_collaborative_filtering/">Lightweight Embeddings with Graph Rewiring for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>量化组合码本+图重连剪枝，在严格存储预算下兼顾 GNN 推荐精度与边缘端推理效率，iFashion 存储降 97.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Graph Transformer&quot;,&quot;位置编码&quot;,&quot;长程协同信号&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/graph_rec/position_aware_graph_transformer_for_recommendation/">Position-aware Graph Transformer for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用四种为交互图定制的节点位置编码把 Transformer 全局建模能力注入 GCN 推荐骨干，在四个数据集上以 Full-Ranking 协议取得最高 27.69% 的 Recall@20 相对提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;噪声视图&quot;,&quot;对称损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/self_supervised_denoising/symmetric_graph_contrastive_learning_against_noisy_views_for_recommendation/">Symmetric Graph Contrastive Learning against Noisy Views for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>定义图增广产生的噪声视图，用对称指数对比损失替代 InfoNCE，使 GCL 对噪声视图鲁棒，全库排序下 NDCG@20 最高提升 12.25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔检索&quot;,&quot;负采样&quot;,&quot;向量索引&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/collaborative_filtering/a_comprehensive_survey_on_retrieval_methods_in_recommender_systems/">A Comprehensive Survey on Retrieval Methods in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个系统梳理推荐召回阶段的综述：从相似度学习、索引机制与训练优化三大维度统一归纳，并在公开数据集上完成跨方法基准评测与工业多路召回案例复盘</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;符号边重加权&quot;,&quot;LLM 表征增强&quot;,&quot;精度-多样性权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/graph_rec/cpgrec_a_balance_oriented_framework_for_personalized_video_game_recommendat/">CPGRec+: A Balance-oriented Framework for Personalized Video Game Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Fisher 分布符号边重加权缓解过平滑、LLM 生成偏好描述精炼表征，在 Steam 双数据集上兼顾精度与多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;连续时间动态图&quot;,&quot;多维画像蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/llm_rec/dynllm_when_large_language_models_meet_dynamic_graph_recommendation/">DynLLM: When Large Language Models Meet Dynamic Graph Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从商品标题生成多维用户画像，经蒸馏注意力注入连续时间动态图，在 Full-Ranking 协议下 Tmall Recall@10 相对最优基线提升 40.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入学习&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;,&quot;哈希与量化压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/collaborative_filtering/embedding_in_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Embedding in Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以「矩阵/序列/图 × 学习范式 × 效率手段」三轴正交框架统一梳理推荐嵌入，归纳哈希/量化/AutoML 压缩路径与 LLM 语义补充，并给出开源复现仓库。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何&quot;,&quot;状态空间模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/sequential_rec/hmamba_hyperbolic_mamba_for_sequential_recommendation/">HMamba: Hyperbolic Mamba for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 Mamba 选择性状态空间搬进 Lorentz 双曲空间，用曲率感知离散化与陀螺向量算子建模层级+时序，在 4 个基准 HR/NDCG 上提升 3–11% 且保持线性复杂度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;超图对比学习&quot;,&quot;相似就诊检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/graph_rec/hypemed_enhancing_medication_recommendations_with_hypergraph_based_patient_/">HypeMed: Enhancing Medication Recommendations with Hypergraph-Based Patient Relationships</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以就诊为超边建模集合级语义，知识感知对比预训练构建可检索空间，就诊条件检索相似病历增强用药推荐，Jaccard 提升 0.4–0.6% 且 DDI 低于真实处方。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回（KG 增强表征学习）&quot;,&quot;知识图谱剪枝&quot;,&quot;双视角重要性评估&quot;,&quot;图稀疏化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/graph_rec/knowledge_graph_pruning_for_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Pruning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双视角重要性评估器为协同知识图谱逐实体打分，剪除至多 98% 三元组仍保精度并提速 12.2×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多兴趣表征&quot;,&quot;表征坍缩与多样性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/sequential_rec/multi_interest_recommendation_a_survey/">Multi-Interest Recommendation: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇多兴趣推荐综述，以隐式/显式建模分类统摄 172 篇文献，把技术栈归一为“兴趣抽取器+兴趣聚合器”两段式框架，并系统梳理去坍缩正则与六大开放挑战。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;级联图卷积&quot;,&quot;对比偏好对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/multi_task_reranking/towards_multi_behavior_multi_task_recommendation_via_behavior_informed_grap/">Towards Multi-Behavior Multi-Task Recommendation via Behavior-informed Graph Embedding Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>BiGEL 在级联图范式上叠加目标→辅助门控反馈、全局上下文增强与对比偏好对齐，JD/UB 全库 Top-5 排序上目标与辅助行为双赢（UB 点击 Prec@5 +101%）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路(召回/排序/重排)&quot;,&quot;高阶连通性&quot;,&quot;综述与分类体系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2023/graph_rec/a_survey_of_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_systems_challenges_method/">A Survey of Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems: Challenges, Methods, and Directions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出阶段/场景/目标/应用四维分类法，围绕图构建、传播聚合、优化与效率四大挑战系统梳理 GNN 推荐，并开源论文-代码索引库</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;图结构学习&quot;,&quot;异质图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2023/social_group_rec/graph_learning_augmented_heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_social_reco/">Graph Learning Augmented Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Graph Learner 在 u2u/u2i/i2i 三路异质全局图上学习并重构图结构，HGNN 捕获跨语义高阶信息，Ciao-5 RMSE 相对最优基线降 4.68%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;解耦表征学习&quot;,&quot;元网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/graph_rec/disentangled_cascaded_graph_convolution_networks_for_multi_behavior_recomme/">Disentangled Cascaded Graph Convolution Networks for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>解耦因子块+元网络个性化迁移+行为级注意力，在Beibei/Tmall全库排序平均提升7.07%/9.00%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;模型更新/遗忘&quot;,&quot;影响函数&quot;,&quot;推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;图卷积溢出影响&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/recommendation_unlearning_via_influence_function/">Recommendation Unlearning via Influence Function</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用影响函数一步更新模型实现推荐遗忘，联合直接损失影响与图卷积溢出影响，剪枝加速后较重训提速 250 倍以上。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Cellular Sheaf&quot;,&quot;Sheaf Diffusion&quot;,&quot;BPR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/graph_rec/sheaf4rec_sheaf_neural_networks_for_graph_based_recommender_systems/">Sheaf4Rec: Sheaf Neural Networks for Graph-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用细胞层 (Cellular Sheaf) 将节点/边表示为向量空间+限制映射,以 Sheaf 拉普拉斯扩散替代单向量 GCN,NDCG@10 最高相对提升 11.29%,且推理耗时反降 2.5%–37%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TWEB&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质信息网络&quot;,&quot;元路径层次注意力&quot;,&quot;强化学习策略梯度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TWEB/2023/graph_rec/reinforced_moocs_concept_recommendation_in_heterogeneous_information_networ/">Reinforced MOOCs Concept Recommendation in Heterogeneous Information Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on the Web">ACM TWEB</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 MOOC 用户-课程-视频-概念构造为异质图，用元路径层次注意力编码用户，并以 REINFORCE 做概念序列推荐，XuetangX 上 HR@5 提升 9.74%+</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2021&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;伪标签 Pseudo-Labelling&quot;,&quot;知识图谱 GNN&quot;,&quot;协同训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2021/cold_start_rec/alleviating_cold_start_problems_in_recommendation_through_pseudo_labelling_/">Alleviating Cold-Start Problems in Recommendation through Pseudo-Labelling over Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在知识图谱上把未观测样本伪标签化为弱正例，配合 KG 路径采样、频率感知负采样与双模型协同训练，显著提升新用户/新物品的召回。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱问答&quot;,&quot;约束建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2021/graph_rec/personalized_food_recommendation_as_constrained_question_answering_over_a_l/">Personalized Food Recommendation as Constrained Question Answering over a Large-scale Food Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将饮食推荐化为FoodKG约束KBQA，用查询扩展、KG增强与约束建模处理数值比较和否定约束，F1较BAMnet绝对提升60.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2021&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表征学习&quot;,&quot;GNN预训练&quot;,&quot;元学习&quot;,&quot;自适应邻居采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2021/cold_start_rec/pre_training_graph_neural_networks_for_cold_start_users_and_items_represent/">Pre-Training Graph Neural Networks for Cold-Start Users and Items Representation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出元学习式GNN预训练、自注意力元聚合器与自适应邻居采样器，直接优化冷启动用户/物品表征，在三个公开数据集的下游推荐上优于GNN基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;元路径注意力&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2021/sequential_rec/temporal_meta_path_guided_explainable_recommendation/">Temporal Meta-path Guided Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用相邻物品间 item-item 元路径替代 RNN 建模时序依赖，配合双层注意力与 MLP，在 Amazon 三数据集上 HR@1 提升 30%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回/精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;元学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/self_supervised_denoising/contrastive_meta_learning_with_behavior_multiplicity_for_recommendation/">Contrastive Meta Learning with Behavior Multiplicity for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用行为感知图传播、跨行为 InfoNCE 与元权重网络解决多行为推荐中目标行为监督稀疏和个性化依赖建模问题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图表征学习&quot;,&quot;分布式训练系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/graph4rec_a_universal_toolkit_with_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_sy/">Graph4Rec: A Universal Toolkit with Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 GNN4Rec 训练统一为五段式流水线，配分布式图引擎与参数服务器，在 ICF/UCF/U2I 召回协议下系统对比 8 类 GNN 并给出工程实践建议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构全局图&quot;,&quot;个性化会话编码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/sequential_rec/heterogeneous_global_graph_neural_networks_for_personalized_session_based_r/">Heterogeneous Global Graph Neural Networks for Personalized Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建用户-物品异构全局图并叠加双粒度偏好门控，在 Last.fm/Xing/Reddit 全库 HR/MRR 上相对 SOTA 提升 1.0%–6.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2020&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;离散哈希码学习&quot;,&quot;直通估计器STE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/learning_to_hash_with_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_systems/">Learning to Hash with Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HashGNN 端到端联合学习 GNN 连续表征与二值哈希码，融合链接重建与 Hamming 排序损失，并以 Guidance STE 消除梯度放大；全库排序 HR@50 较 GraphSage 提升 4–10%，检索提速 2.5–3.6 倍。</p>
</dd>
