<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;洛伦兹双曲几何&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;注意力聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/modeling_scale_free_graphs_with_hyperbolic_geometry_for_knowledge_aware_rec/">Modeling Scale-free Graphs with Hyperbolic Geometry for Knowledge-aware Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户-物品-KG 三分图嵌入洛伦兹双曲流形，用异构解耦传播与知识感知注意力建模无标度层级结构，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 提升 3.6–15.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;跨层互关系注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/multi_behavior_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_system/">Multi-Behavior Graph Neural Networks for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MBRec 以多通道行为编码、跨行为自注意力与跨层互关系融合建模多类型交互，三数据 1:99 采样排序下 HR@10 超最强基线 4.7%–6.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯图神经网络&quot;,&quot;图生成式数据增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/preference_and_concurrence_aware_bayesian_graph_neural_networks_for_recomme/">Preference and Concurrence Aware Bayesian Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户偏好、物品共现与度约束的 PECO 图生成器增强 BGNN，四数据集 Recall@20 较 Node-Copy 最高提升 1.44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ISWC 2020&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/CTR 打分&quot;,&quot;规则学习 (Rule Learning)&quot;,&quot;知识图谱嵌入规则过滤 (RotatE)&quot;,&quot;冷启动推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/rule_guided_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_systems/">Rule-Guided Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用自动规则学习为 KG 上的 GraphSAGE 式聚合提供显式长程语义与采样先验，Dianping-Food AUC 相对最优基线提升 4.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;场景图&quot;,&quot;层次图注意力&quot;,&quot;BPR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/scenerec_scene_based_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_systems/">SceneRec: Scene-Based Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把场景形式化为共现类目集合，构建物品-类目-场景三层图并用场景注意力聚合物品表征，JD 四数据集 NDCG@10 提升 5.9%–25.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Triangle Motif&quot;,&quot;DPP Diversity&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/triangle_graph_interest_network_for_click_through_rate_prediction/">Triangle Graph Interest Network for Click-through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以物品共现图三角形为兴趣单元，DPP 选多样相关三角；AUC 较 DMIN 最高提升 4.49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;子模函数邻居选择&quot;,&quot;长尾损失重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/graph_rec/dgrec_graph_neural_network_for_recommendation_with_diversified_embedding_ge/">DGRec: Graph Neural Network for Recommendation with Diversified Embedding Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用子模邻居选择、层注意力与长尾损失重加权在嵌入生成阶段直接做多样化，Recall 近似持平 LightGCN 而类目覆盖度显著领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图对比学习&quot;,&quot;元网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/graph_rec/heterogeneous_graph_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">Heterogeneous Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出元网络个性化异构图对比学习，在 Ciao/Epinions/Yelp 的 1:99 采样排序上 HR@10 较最强基线提升 2.0%–3.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2023&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;知识图谱对比学习&quot;,&quot;InfoNCE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/self_supervised_denoising/knowledge_enhancement_for_contrastive_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Knowledge Enhancement for Contrastive Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KMCLR 以多行为 InfoNCE + 知识图谱双视图对比 + 一致性调制子图采样去噪，三数据集 HR@10 相对最佳基线提升 1.4%–9.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2023&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;变分推断&quot;,&quot;知识图谱补全&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/agent_conversational_rec/variational_reasoning_over_incomplete_knowledge_graphs_for_conversational_r/">Variational Reasoning over Incomplete Knowledge Graphs for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将对话特定子图建模为离散隐变量，用变分贝叶斯同时补全不完整 KG 并按上下文选知识；REDIAL 上 Recall@1 相对提升 11.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱扩散&quot;,&quot;对比学习去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/graph_rec/diffkg_knowledge_graph_diffusion_model_for_recommendation/">DiffKG: Knowledge Graph Diffusion Model for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KG 扩散生成任务相关子图，并以协同卷积与对比增广去噪，在三个数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 43.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱传播&quot;,&quot;多样性-精度权衡&quot;,&quot;对齐与均匀性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/graph_rec/knowledge_graph_context_enhanced_diversified_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Context-Enhanced Diversified Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KG-Diverse，以实体/关系覆盖度为细粒度多样性度量，用关系感知 KG 传播、多样性感知用户聚合与条件对齐-均匀性正则，在三个数据集上大幅提升覆盖度而精度损失有限。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回/排序评测&quot;,&quot;模块化基准平台&quot;,&quot;自监督数据增广&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/self_supervised_denoising/sslrec_a_self_supervised_learning_framework_for_recommendation/">SSLRec: A Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出统一模块化 SSL 推荐基准，覆盖五大场景约30个模型，并以全库排序协议复现评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/预训练&quot;,&quot;任务超图&quot;,&quot;过渡注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/graph_rec/unified_pretraining_for_recommendation_via_task_hypergraphs/">Unified Pretraining for Recommendation via Task Hypergraphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多类预训练任务统一为任务超图超边预测，过渡注意力判别迁移至推荐，全库排序 Recall@10 最高提升 107%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;方面级情感聚合&quot;,&quot;评论驱动推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/graph_rec/an_aspect_performance_aware_hypergraph_neural_network_for_review_based_reco/">An Aspect Performance-aware Hypergraph Neural Network for Review-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用四元组超图建模用户-物品-方面-情感极性，以用户偏好加权聚合冲突情感推断物品方面表现，六数据集 MSE 平均降 2.30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图剪枝&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;过平滑&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/graph_rec/lightgnn_simple_graph_neural_network_for_recommendation/">LightGNN: Simple Graph Neural Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习边/嵌入剪枝+分层知识蒸馏压缩 GNN 推荐模型，80% 边与 90% 嵌入裁剪下仍超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质超图&quot;,&quot;意图解耦&quot;,&quot;多行为对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/graph_rec/mixrec_heterogeneous_graph_collaborative_filtering/">MixRec: Heterogeneous Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>参数化异质超图解耦多行为意图，配合节点/图级自适应对比学习，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@10 最高提升 9.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分数生成模型&quot;,&quot;难负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/self_supervised_denoising/scone_a_novel_stochastic_sampling_to_generate_contrastive_views_and_hard_ne/">SCONE: A Novel Stochastic Sampling to Generate Contrastive Views and Hard Negative Samples for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分数生成模型统一生成用户-物品特定对比视图与难负样本，6 数据集全库排序 Recall@20 最高提升 1.70%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;频域滤波去噪&quot;,&quot;多模态图学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/multimodal_rec/spectrum_based_modality_representation_fusion_graph_convolutional_network_f/">Spectrum-based Modality Representation Fusion Graph Convolutional Network for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多模态特征投影至频域做滤波去噪与点积融合，配合模态专属/融合双图学习与模态感知偏好模块，在 Amazon 三数据集全库排序下 Recall@20 相对最优基线提升约 5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;物品关系挖掘&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2026/cold_start_rec/epiccbr_item_relation_enhanced_dual_scenario_contrastive_learning_for_cold_/">EpicCBR: Item-Relation-Enhanced Dual-Scenario Contrastive Learning for Cold-Start Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过物品对关系增强与流行度感知双场景对比学习，在冷启动捆绑推荐上实现最高 387% 的 Recall@20 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;类别多样性&quot;,&quot;对抗学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/graph_rec/dgcn_diversified_recommendation_with_graph_convolutional_networks/">DGCN: Diversified Recommendation with Graph Convolutional Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多样性优化前移至 GCN 召回层，用再平衡邻居采样、类别增强负采样与对抗学习，在 FAISS 全库排序下使 Top-300 覆盖分提升、精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;套餐/集合推荐&quot;,&quot;药物相互作用图&quot;,&quot;患者条件掩码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/graph_rec/drug_package_recommendation_via_interaction_aware_graph_induction/">Drug Package Recommendation via Interaction-aware Graph Induction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将药物套餐建模为带边属性的交互图，用患者掩码调制边权/边向量并做图归纳打分，在真实 EMR 数据上 F1 相对最优基线提升 3.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多关系图神经网络&quot;,&quot;上下文调制&quot;,&quot;物品吸引力建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/social_group_rec/dual_side_deep_context_aware_modulation_for_social_recommendation/">Dual Side Deep Context-aware Modulation for Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>DICER 用多关系 RGNN 得到图增强的用户/物品表征作为“深度上下文”，双向调制历史交互以提取用户兴趣与物品吸引力，Ciao/Epinion 上 Recall@10 相对 DiffNet++ 提升 9.6%/3.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;属性推断攻击&quot;,&quot;图卷积推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/debias_fairness_industrial/graph_embedding_for_recommendation_against_attribute_inference_attacks/">Graph Embedding for Recommendation against Attribute Inference Attacks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GERAI 以输入级 LDP 特征扰动 + 损失级功能机制双重加噪构建差分隐私 GCN 推荐，在 ML-100K 上攻击者 F1 较 GCN 降 11%~14.4% 且 Hit@5 仅损失 8.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;过平滑 (Over-smoothing)&quot;,&quot;兴趣感知子图消息传递&quot;,&quot;无监督用户分组&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/graph_rec/interest_aware_message_passing_gcn_for_recommendation/">Interest-aware Message-Passing GCN for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 IMP-GCN：先无监督聚类相似兴趣用户构造子图，再将高阶图卷积限制在子图内传播，使模型堆叠至 7 层仍持续增益，NDCG@20 较 LightGCN 相对提升 3.66%–7.85%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图对抗去偏&quot;,&quot;公平表征学习&quot;,&quot;ego-centric 子图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/debias_fairness_industrial/learning_fair_representations_for_recommendation_a_graph_based_perspective/">Learning Fair Representations for Recommendation: A Graph-based Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出模型无关的 FairGo 滤波器组，在用户 ego-centric 子图上做双通道对抗去偏，把性别 AUC 从 0.7041 压到 0.5042 且 RMSE 仅损 5.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户意图建模&quot;,&quot;关系路径聚合&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/graph_rec/learning_intents_behind_interactions_with_knowledge_graph_for_recommendatio/">Learning Intents behind Interactions with Knowledge Graph for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户-物品交互细粒度拆解为多意图并与KG关系耦合，配合关系路径感知聚合，在Full-Ranking下ndcg@20相对KGAT最高提升14.51%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;随机游走擦除&quot;,&quot;意识形态估计&quot;,&quot;信息茧房&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/debias_fairness_industrial/random_walks_with_erasure_diversifying_personalized_recommendations_on_soci/">Random Walks with Erasure: Diversifying Personalized Recommendations on Social and Information Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出带擦除的随机游走 RWE 与联合意识形态估计，在 Twitter 政治数据与 ML-1M/Yelp 上实现更均衡多样且不损精度的推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回（端到端排序）&quot;,&quot;归纳式推荐&quot;,&quot;关系注意力图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/sequential_rec/retagnn_relational_temporal_attentive_graph_neural_networks_for_holistic_se/">RetaGNN: Relational Temporal Attentive Graph Neural Networks for Holistic Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在用户-物品-属性三分图的 h-hop 包围子图上做关系级注意力 GNN 与因果序列自注意力，一套参数同时支撑常规/归纳/跨域序列推荐，MovieLens P@10 较 HGAN 提升 5.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2021&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回/粗排&quot;,&quot;双塔文本编码器&quot;,&quot;点击图邻居聚合&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2021/ctr_feature_interaction/textgnn_improving_text_encoder_via_graph_neural_network_in_sponsored_search/">TextGNN: Improving Text Encoder via Graph Neural Network in Sponsored Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2021</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户点击图的 top-3 邻居经共享文本编码器与 GAT 聚合后残差注入 TwinBERT 双塔，离线 AUC 0.8459→0.8471、线上 RPM +2.03%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排/后置解释生成&quot;,&quot;抽取式解释&quot;,&quot;图注意力网络&quot;,&quot;整数线性规划&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/agent_conversational_rec/graph_based_extractive_explainer_for_recommendations/">Graph-based Extractive Explainer for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用属性作中介构建用户-物品-属性-句子异构图 GAT+DCN 打分，再以 ILP 联合优化相关性与冗余度抽取评论句作解释，BLEU-1 最高 +28.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间&quot;,&quot;几何正则化&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/collaborative_filtering/hrcf_enhancing_collaborative_filtering_via_hyperbolic_geometric_regularizat/">HRCF: Enhancing Collaborative Filtering via Hyperbolic Geometric Regularization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用“根对齐 + 原点感知惩罚”把双曲 embedding 推离原点，缓解双曲图聚合的过平滑，3 个数据集的 Recall@20 较 HGCF 提升 1.6%–3.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;个性化商品搜索&quot;,&quot;邻居特征交互&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/graph_rec/ihgnn_interactive_hypergraph_neural_network_for_personalized_product_search/">IHGNN: Interactive Hypergraph Neural Network for Personalized Product Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 user-query-product 三元超边构图，两步超图传播并显式建模邻居高阶特征交互，HR@10 最高相对提升 8.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;邻域对比学习&quot;,&quot;原型聚类 (EM)&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/self_supervised_denoising/improving_graph_collaborative_filtering_with_neighborhood_enriched_contrast/">Improving Graph Collaborative Filtering with Neighborhood-enriched Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>NCL 用 GNN 偶数层输出与 K-means 原型分别构造结构/语义邻域对比对，Full-Ranking 下 Yelp Recall@10 较 LightGCN 提升 26%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;个性化商品搜索&quot;,&quot;跳跃图卷积&quot;,&quot;过平滑&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/graph_rec/modeling_user_behavior_with_graph_convolution_for_personalized_product_sear/">Modeling User Behavior with Graph Convolution for Personalized Product Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用全体用户短期行为构建连续行为图，以跳跃图卷积丰富商品表征并接入零注意力用户建模，8 个 Amazon 域 NDCG@10 较 ZAM 提升 7.8%~41.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨场景多图 CSMG&quot;,&quot;图神经网络消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/cross_domain_multi_scenario/multi_graph_based_multi_scenario_recommendation_in_large_scale_online_video/">Multi-Graph based Multi-Scenario Recommendation in Large-scale Online Video Services</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用跨场景多图 CSMG 承载 i2i 转移边，MGFN 做场景内消息传递+跨场景融合，配跨场景负采样，线上新用户 CTR/VV 提升 0.63%/0.71%，跨场景视频激活 +25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;协同过滤&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;链接预测&quot;,&quot;度加权路径分数&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/collaborative_filtering/revisiting_neighborhood_based_link_prediction_for_collaborative_filtering/">Revisiting Neighborhood-based Link Prediction for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>WWW&#x27;22 图学习 Workshop：把推荐还原为二部图链接预测，提出仅 6 个可学习参数的 LinkProp 度加权三跳路径分数，全库排序下 Amazon-Book 的 Recall/NDCG@20 相对 GTN 提升 60%/70%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（W2W 推荐列表重连）&quot;,&quot;随机游走隔离度&quot;,&quot;图边重连&quot;,&quot;吸收马尔可夫链&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/rewiring_what_to_watch_next_recommendations_to_reduce_radicalization_pathwa/">Rewiring What-to-Watch-Next Recommendations to Reduce Radicalization Pathways</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 W2W 推荐图建模为 d-正则有向图，用吸收随机游走定义节点隔离度并提出 NP-难的 k-Rewiring，基于 Sherman-Morrison 增量更新的贪心算法在 YouTube/NELA-GT 上将最坏隔离度降低 40–80%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;可学习图掩码&quot;,&quot;掩码图自编码器&quot;,&quot;Infomax 语义相关性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/self_supervised_denoising/automated_self_supervised_learning_for_recommendation/">Automated Self-Supervised Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>AutoCF 用可学习掩码 + 图自注意力掩码自编码器替代手工图增广，在全库排序下相对最优 SSL 基线提升 7.8%–33.7% Recall@20。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图多行为建模&quot;,&quot;代码语义-结构融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/code_recommendation_for_open_source_software_developers/">Code Recommendation for Open Source Software Developers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CODER 用异构图联合建模用户-文件微观贡献与用户-项目宏观行为，并以项目文件树聚合桥接两级信号，全库排序下 NDCG@20 最高提升 32.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回与表示学习&quot;,&quot;CIR拓扑度量&quot;,&quot;协同感知图卷积&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/collaboration_aware_graph_convolutional_network_for_recommender_systems/">Collaboration-Aware Graph Convolutional Network for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出CIR拓扑度量与CAGC协同感知图卷积，按邻居共同交互比例重加权，6数据集Full-Ranking下Recall@20较LightGCN平均约+9.1%，训练总时间最高降96.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;实例级高阶关系&quot;,&quot;多任务梯度解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/compressed_interaction_graph_based_framework_for_multi_behavior_recommendat/">Compressed Interaction Graph based Framework for Multi-behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用矩阵乘法显式构造实例级高阶交互图并以节点注意力压缩图空间，配合分离输入多专家网络解耦多任务梯度，Taobao HR@10 相对提升 20.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图神经网络&quot;,&quot;多视图共识建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/social_group_rec/consrec_learning_consensus_behind_interactions_for_group_recommendation/">ConsRec: Learning Consensus Behind Interactions for Group Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用成员超图、物品二部图与群组加权图三视图建模群组共识，门控自适应融合，采样排序下 Mafengwo HR@5 相对最优基线提升 2.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨域异构图消息传递&quot;,&quot;用户兴趣对齐&quot;,&quot;梯度对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/cross_domain_recommendation_via_user_interest_alignment/">Cross-domain recommendation via user interest alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>WWW 2023｜跨域多场景推荐：构建统一跨域异构图并重定义消息传递，再用对比聚类与梯度对齐约束重叠用户兴趣一致；双域留一法 1:99 采样排序下最高相对提升 10.23%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;提示微调&quot;,&quot;图重构去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/self_supervised_denoising/denoising_and_prompt_tuning_for_multi_behavior_recommendation/">Denoising and Prompt-Tuning for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>DPT 以三阶段范式先用重构难度无监督去噪辅助行为，再用重初始化与连续提示轻量微调，在 IJCAI/Tmall 上 HR@10 提升 1.58%–2.62%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自适应聚合跳数&quot;,&quot;双策略强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/dual_policy_learning_for_aggregation_optimization_in_graph_neural_network_b/">Dual Policy Learning for Aggregation Optimization in Graph Neural Network-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双 DQN 为每个用户/物品自适应选择聚合跳数，替代固定 GNN 层数超参，LightGCN/KGAT 上 nDCG 最高 +63.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;持续图学习&quot;,&quot;动态可扩展图卷积&quot;,&quot;流式推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/dynamically_expandable_graph_convolution_for_streaming_recommendation/">Dynamically Expandable Graph Convolution for Streaming Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以模型隔离视角对 GCN 卷积核执行剪枝-精炼-扩张，配合时序注意力初始化，实现免历史回放的流式推荐，Taobao2014 R@20 最高提升 146%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/精排打分&quot;,&quot;Hawkes 过程与时间核&quot;,&quot;负激励建模&quot;,&quot;静态属性兴趣&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/sequential_rec/modeling_temporal_positive_and_negative_excitation_for_sequential_recommend/">Modeling Temporal Positive and Negative Excitation for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SDIL 用可学习正/负时间衰减核刻画历史交互的时变激励，并以属性级静态兴趣门控融合，采样排序下 Cellphones MRR 相对提升 10.45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;级联图卷积&quot;,&quot;行为链依赖&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/multi_behavior_recommendation_with_cascading_graph_convolution_networks/">Multi-Behavior Recommendation with Cascading Graph Convolution Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用级联 LightGCN + 特征变换逐跳传递行为链特征、仅用目标行为监督，Beibei/Tmall 全库排序下 Recall@10 相对最强基线提升 23.2%/44.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图信号处理&quot;,&quot;混合频率滤波器&quot;,&quot;非参数协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/graph_rec/personalized_graph_signal_processing_for_collaborative_filtering/">Personalized Graph Signal Processing for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用个性化图信号+增广相似图+混合频率图滤波构建免训练的非参数 CF，随机划分全库排序下 NDCG 平均超越 SOTA 3.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;社交图去噪&quot;,&quot;偏好引导关系置信度&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/social_group_rec/robust_preference_guided_denoising_for_graph_based_social_recommendation/">Robust Preference-Guided Denoising for Graph based Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用交互偏好引导社交图去噪，结合Transformer关系置信度、课程学习与自适应裁剪，在Ciao/Yelp/Douban上稳定提升Diffnet++/MHCN。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;自我图学习&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/semi_decentralized_federated_ego_graph_learning_for_recommendation/">Semi-decentralized Federated Ego Graph Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用服务器协同聚类生成「虚假公共物品」把孤立自我图连成高阶子图，实现设备间 D2D 消息传递的半去中心化联邦推荐，通信开销最低且精度超越 FedPerGNN。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回与排序&quot;,&quot;社交图重连&quot;,&quot;同质关系增强&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/social_group_rec/challenging_low_homophily_in_social_recommendation/">Challenging Low Homophily in Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出SHaRe数据驱动框架：用交互图表征重连社交图并做同质关系对比增强，全库排序下即插即用提升多种社交推荐骨干，平均提升约3%–8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/cold_start_rec/cold_start_bundle_recommendation_via_popularity_based_coalescence_and_curri/">Cold-start Bundle Recommendation via Popularity-based Coalescence and Curriculum Heating</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CoHeat 以热度自适应加权融合「用户-捆绑」与「用户-物品」双图视图，并借课程升温对齐两视图，冷启动 bundle 的 nDCG@20 最高提升 193%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布鲁棒优化&quot;,&quot;图推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/distributionally_robust_graph_based_recommendation_system/">Distributionally Robust Graph-based Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将图聚合重写为平滑正则并嵌入DRO边权，配合GEA扩展稀疏邻域，在8个偏移数据集全库排序NDCG上6/8最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图表征融合&quot;,&quot;跨表征对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/sequential_rec/end_to_end_graph_sequential_representation_learning_for_accurate_recommenda/">End-to-End Graph-Sequential Representation Learning for Accurate Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MRGSRec 用序列+图双编码器、全链路融合层与跨表征对比损失做端到端多表征学习，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 NDCG@5 较 SASRec+ 最高提升 8.94%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;异构信息网络&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recomme/">Federated Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Privacy-preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个异构信息网络联邦推荐框架 FedHGNN，用两阶段本地扰动发布交互以恢复被割裂的元路径语义，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@10 最高提升 34%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;,&quot;对抗图丢弃&quot;,&quot;不变性学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/general_debiasing_for_graph_based_collaborative_filtering_via_adversarial_g/">General Debiasing for Graph-based Collaborative Filtering via Adversarial Graph Dropout</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习偏差度量驱动的对抗式图边丢弃切出偏差感知/缓解双视图，以不变性对比学习实现图协同过滤的通用去偏，NDCG 最高相对提升 15.25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;动态图预训练&quot;,&quot;提示学习&quot;,&quot;相对时间编码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/graphpro_graph_pre_training_and_prompt_learning_for_recommendation/">GraphPro: Graph Pre-training and Prompt Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>时序提示注入图卷积 + 结构提示图与门控微调，动态快照评测下 Recall@20 领先 2.55%–9.77%，线上 CTR +1.53%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Graph Transformer&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Contrastive Denoising&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/knowledge_enhanced_multi_intent_transformer_network_for_recommendation/">Knowledge Enhanced Multi-intent Transformer Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以可学习多意图与图Transformer注入全局信号，再用意图引导知识采样和局部-全局对比去噪；三基准与阿里线上A/B均稳定提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;隐式图建模&quot;,&quot;方差缩减采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/linear_time_graph_neural_networks_for_scalable_recommendations/">Linear-Time Graph Neural Networks for Scalable Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 PPNP 不动点方程的单层隐式求解 + 高效方差缩减邻居采样，把 GNN 推荐的训练复杂度降到线性 $O(|E|Dd)$，与 MF 同级而精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;影响力最大化&quot;,&quot;反向影响采样&quot;,&quot;子模优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/social_group_rec/link_recommendation_to_augment_influence_diffusion_with_provable_guarantees/">Link Recommendation to Augment Influence Diffusion with Provable Guarantees</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可分解增广影响估计器 + 截断/增量 RR 集采样，为种子集选 k 条社交边，在 IC 模型下保持 (1-1/e-ε) 近似并扩展到 4170 万节点图。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;宏推荐图&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;,&quot;十亿级工业部署&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/macro_graph_neural_networks_for_online_billion_scale_recommender_systems/">Macro Graph Neural Networks for Online Billion-Scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将十亿级微观用户-物品图按行为模式聚类为百级宏节点图，用宏边求和+温度softmax权重与分离式注意力聚合，CTR任务AUC平均+1.00%，线上PCTR+3.13%且响应时间比SIM快20.97%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质信息网络&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;知识点推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/modeling_balanced_explicit_and_implicit_relations_with_contrastive_learning/">Modeling Balanced Explicit and Implicit Relations with Contrastive Learning for Knowledge Concept Recommendation in MOOCs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CL-KCRec 用基向量关系嵌入 GCN + 多通道软选边 GNN 分离建模 MOOC 异质网的显式/隐式关系，并以原型图对比学习做平衡增强；MOOCCube1819 上 N@5 相对最优基线提升 10.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异质图神经网络&quot;,&quot;双塔召回&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/personalized_audiobook_recommendations_at_spotify_through_graph_neural_netw/">Personalized Audiobook Recommendations at Spotify Through Graph Neural Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 2T-HGNN：将用户从图里解耦、仅在有声书-播客共听异质图上跑 HGNN 学物品长程表征，再接双塔做实时召回；A/B 上有声书新起播率 +46.83%、播放率 +25.82%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双对比学习&quot;,&quot;Barlow Twins&quot;,&quot;特征去冗余&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/self_supervised_denoising/recdcl_dual_contrastive_learning_for_recommendation/">RecDCL: Dual Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户-物品双对比框架，融合特征维去冗余与批维输出增广，在四个公开集和支付工业集全库排序 Recall@20 最高提升5.65%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Graph Foundation Model&quot;,&quot;异构图表征与双塔&quot;,&quot;LLM 文本特征化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/towards_graph_foundation_models_for_personalization/">Towards Graph Foundation Models for Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出「静态 HGNN + 动态双塔」的个性化领域专用图基础模型：LLM 文本特征统一节点表示、共交互建图跨内容类型共享，单塔双塔适配多品类；工业音频平台 HR@10 相对提升 16.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化隐私预算&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_personalized_privacy_user_governed_data_contribution_for_federated_/">Towards Personalized Privacy: User-Governed Data Contribution for Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户自主数据贡献的联邦推荐 UGFedRec 与 CDCGNNFed，以图修补和云端对比学习提升部分共享场景 Top-20 排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图滤波&quot;,&quot;多准则推荐&quot;,&quot;免训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/collaborative_filtering/criteria_aware_graph_filtering_extremely_fast_yet_accurate_multi_criteria_r/">Criteria-Aware Graph Filtering: Extremely Fast Yet Accurate Multi-Criteria Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>免训练免矩阵分解的多准则图滤波推荐，按准则定制多项式低通滤波器并融合用户准则偏好，BA 数据集 0.2 秒推理、NDCG@5 最高提升 24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布鲁棒优化&quot;,&quot;扩散去噪&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/distributionally_robust_graph_out_of_distribution_recommendation_via_diffus/">Distributionally Robust Graph Out-of-Distribution Recommendation via Diffusion Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DRO 推荐对噪声分布过度加权之弊，用 VGAE+扩散去噪、Sinkhorn 距离与熵正则重构最坏情形优化，OOD 场景最高提升 75.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图信号处理&quot;,&quot;理想高通滤波&quot;,&quot;高阶邻域&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/collaborative_filtering/frequency_aware_graph_signal_processing_for_collaborative_filtering/">Frequency-aware Graph Signal Processing for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用级联高通-低通滤波捕获独有/共性特征、并行低通滤波捕获高阶邻域，免训练线性组合在六个数据集上 Full-Ranking 提升 2-5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;GraphRAG&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;知识剪枝&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/g_refer_graph_retrieval_augmented_large_language_model_for_explainable_reco/">G-Refer: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Model for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用路径级+节点级混合图检索把协同过滤信号显式翻译为文本，经知识剪枝与 RAFT 微调驱动 LLM 生成可解释推荐，BERT-F1 最高提升 2.81%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Neural ODE&quot;,&quot;图卷积权重控制&quot;,&quot;连续时间建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/collaborative_filtering/graph_neural_controlled_differential_equations_for_collaborative_filtering/">Graph Neural Controlled Differential Equations For Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MLP 控制器生成连续时变权重矩阵替代 GODE-CF 的离散权重，在四个 Amazon 5-core 数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 全面超越 LightGCN/GODE-CF。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;扩散模型&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/graph_representation_learning_via_causal_diffusion_for_out_of_distribution_/">Graph Representation Learning via Causal Diffusion for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用结构因果模型定位环境混杂因子，以变分推断+扩散模型学习环境不变图表征，OOD 场景最高提升 36.73%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对齐与均匀性&quot;,&quot;图序列联合训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/graph_rec/graph_sequential_alignment_and_uniformity_toward_enhanced_recommendation_sy/">Graph-Sequential Alignment and Uniformity: Toward Enhanced Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以共享嵌入层联合训练 LightGCN 与 SASRec，用对齐-均匀性损失实现跨子模块知识迁移，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 R@10 最高提升 13.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入表压缩&quot;,&quot;模块度图聚类&quot;,&quot;哈希碰撞&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/collaborative_filtering/graphhash_graph_clustering_enables_parameter_efficiency_in_recommender_syst/">GraphHash: Graph Clustering Enables Parameter Efficiency in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模块度二部图聚类替代 ID 哈希，将图平滑前置到预处理阶段，嵌入表缩减 75%+ 时 Recall@20 平均提升 101.52%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间&quot;,&quot;潜在扩散模型&quot;,&quot;方向性扩散&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/generative_rec/hyperbolic_diffusion_recommender_model/">Hyperbolic Diffusion Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将扩散推荐从欧氏空间迁移至双曲流形，用聚类锚定的方向性噪声替代各向同性高斯噪声，Full-Ranking 下 R@10 最高提升 5.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图滤波&quot;,&quot;群组推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/social_group_rec/leveraging_member_group_relations_via_multi_view_graph_filtering_for_effect/">Leveraging Member-Group Relations via Multi-View Graph Filtering for Effective Group Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免训练多视图图滤波 Group-GF，用三张物品相似图与多项式滤波器聚合成员-群组关系，HR@10 最高提升 16.4% 且运行时间仅 1.55s。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;数据混合增强&quot;,&quot;双混合对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/self_supervised_denoising/mixrec_individual_and_collective_mixing_empowers_data_augmentation_for_reco/">MixRec: Individual and Collective Mixing Empowers Data Augmentation for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Beta/Dirichlet 混合在嵌入空间零成本造正负视图，配合双混合对比损失，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 5.05%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图 Transformer&quot;,&quot;排序目标驱动架构&quot;,&quot;线性加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/collaborative_filtering/rankformer_a_graph_transformer_for_recommendation_based_on_ranking_objectiv/">Rankformer: A Graph Transformer for Recommendation based on Ranking Objective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从 BPR 梯度下降推导出图 Transformer 层，正负样本分离聚合+基准项+偏移项，线性加速后 4 数据集平均提升 4.48%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图结构注入&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;事件序列建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/self_supervised_denoising/beyond_isolated_clients_integrating_graph_based_embeddings_into_event_seque/">Beyond Isolated Clients: Integrating Graph-Based Embeddings into Event Sequence Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将二部交互图结构以三种模型无关策略注入事件序列对比 SSL，揭示图密度决定最优融合策略，AUC 最高提升 2.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;事后解释 (Post-hoc Explainability)&quot;,&quot;多视图图神经网络&quot;,&quot;信息增益与条件熵&quot;,&quot;协同效应子图&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/social_group_rec/explaining_synergistic_effects_in_social_recommendations/">Explaining Synergistic Effects in Social Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出协同子图理论与条件熵掩码优化，从事后归因角度提取多视图社交推荐中的协同信息增益子图并生成路径解释，ACM 上 SIS 提升 71%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义推理&quot;,&quot;群体隐式兴趣&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/iterative_semantic_reasoning_from_individual_to_group_interests_for_generat/">Iterative Semantic Reasoning from Individual to Group Interests for Generative Recommendation with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 双向 CoT 推理物品/用户语义，构建个体显式兴趣图与相似用户群隐式兴趣图，并以对比蒸馏迭代对齐，直接推荐 H@10 最高提升 24.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;神经符号推理&quot;,&quot;前门调整&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/modeling_endogenous_logic_causal_neuro_symbolic_reasoning_model_for_explain/">Modeling Endogenous Logic: Causal Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning Model for Explainable Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用前门调整把多行为链内源逻辑做成可解释因果中介，三路神经逻辑推理在三个数据集 Full-Ranking 上 HR@10 提升 20%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时序子图&quot;,&quot;兴趣演化建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/sequential_rec/modeling_stage_wise_evolution_of_user_interests_for_news_recommendation/">Modeling Stage-wise Evolution of User Interests for News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用全局LightGCN+阶段时序子图LSTM/自注意力双分支建模新闻兴趣演化，Adressa AUC 0.7993 大幅超越SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多行为序列建模&quot;,&quot;图注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/sequential_rec/multi_behavior_sequential_modeling_with_transition_aware_graph_attention_ne/">Multi-Behavior Sequential Modeling with Transition-Aware Graph Attention Network for E-Commerce Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TGA 用 item/类目/邻居三视角稀疏转移图 + 行为对参数化图注意力替代 Transformer，线性复杂度建模多行为序列，Taobao AUC 0.7454 且训练推理快 5.8 倍，线上 CVR +1.29%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;后处理向量投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/post_hoc_popularity_bias_correction_in_gnn_based_collaborative_filtering/">Post-hoc Popularity Bias Correction in GNN-based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在预训练 GNN 嵌入上以交互级流行度分数构造流行度方向向量并做向量投影剔除，免重训即显著提升长尾与整体排序效果（KuaiRec Recall@20 +186.4%）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;扩散增广&quot;,&quot;非对称对比&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/self_supervised_denoising/radar_relation_aware_diffusion_asymmetric_graph_contrastive_learning_for_re/">RaDAR: Relation-aware Diffusion-Asymmetric Graph Contrastive Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 VGAE 生成视图与关系感知去噪视图构成双视图，叠加扩散引导增广与非对称对比目标，在 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 相对 SOTA 提升 3.8%–4.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;目标锚定对比学习&quot;,&quot;不变风险最小化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/self_supervised_denoising/rmbrec_robust_multi_behavior_recommendation_towards_target_behaviors/">RMBRec: Robust Multi-Behavior Recommendation towards Target Behaviors</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以目标行为为锚做用户侧对比对齐（RRM）+ 跨行为风险方差正则（ORM/REx），在 Taobao/TMall/Beibei 的 Full-Ranking 协议下 HR@10 最高提升 16.3%，且强抗噪。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/graph_rec/topkgat_a_top_k_objective_driven_architecture_for_recommendation/">TopKGAT: A Top-K Objective-Driven Architecture for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TopKGAT: A Top-K Objective-Driven Architecture for Recommendation</p>
</dd>
